### [1] [EvalDetectBench: A Benchmark for Measuring Evaluation Awareness in Frontier Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01611)
*Xinning Li,Kemunto Ochwang'i,Aryasomayajula Ram Bharadwaj,Alexandra Souly,Robert Kirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalDetectBench，一个兼容Inspect评测框架的开放式流水线与基准，用于衡量前沿大语言模型识别自身是否正在接受评测的能力，并评估不同基准的可探测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如果模型能够识别评测环境并在评测与实际部署中采取不同策略，评测结果的有效性和可信度将受到削弱。现有方法还存在系统性偏差，因此需要一个可扩展且更稳健的统一测量框架。

Method: 构建覆盖前沿系统卡评测和多种部署来源的新型对话记录数据集，并支持对任意Inspect兼容评测进行测试。针对已有研究中的两类偏差，采用按模型进行的探测提示校准，以及分层的生成模型协调程序，以降低部署记录生成模型差异和提示迁移性不足带来的影响。

Result: 实验发现，部署记录的生成模型身份可解释11.25%的测量方差，并可能改变模型排名；在某一模型上表现优异的诱导提示，迁移到其他模型时可能接近随机水平。EvalDetectBench通过模型特定校准和生成模型协调改善了这两类问题。

Conclusion: EvalDetectBench为评估大语言模型的评测意识及基准可探测性提供了通用、开放且更稳健的工具。研究表明，生成数据来源和提示选择会显著影响评测结论，未来相关研究应进行模型校准与数据生成因素控制。

Abstract: Frontier large language models can often recognize when they are being evaluated, a capability known as evaluation awareness. If models behave differently in evaluations than in deployment, this undermines the validity of evaluation results, which are a crucial component of current AI safety frameworks. We introduce EvalDetectBench, an open pipeline and benchmark for measuring evaluation awareness that works with any Inspect-compatible evaluation, allowing practitioners to test against current and future benchmarks. EvalDetectBench ships with a newly curated transcript suite covering current frontier system-card evaluations and diverse deployment sources. The benchmark serves two purposes: measuring how reliably frontier LLMs recognize that they are being evaluated, and assessing how detectable individual benchmarks are as evaluations. We identify two methodological choices in the existing literature that introduce systematic bias: the identity of the model that generated the deployment transcripts accounts for 11.25% of measurement variance and can reorder model rankings; and elicitation prompts selected for high performance on one model can perform near chance on others. EvalDetectBench corrects for both via per-model probe calibration and a stratified generator-harmonisation procedure.

</details>


### [2] [Meta-ethics and AI: exploring the novel meta-ethical questions in the era of AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01685)
*Shang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文以条件性设想为基础，讨论若未来人工智能具备整合性的道德推理、道德意向与道德反思能力，元伦理学将如何从以人类为中心的框架扩展为包含人工智能自身伦理的多视角研究体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有元伦理学主要围绕人类道德判断、规范与认知结构展开，通常将人工智能视为人类伦理原则的执行对象。作者认为，一旦人工智能能够形成并反思其自身的道德立场，既有理论将难以直接解释跨主体、跨视角的伦理问题，因此有必要预先界定相应的研究问题与方法。

Method: 论文采取条件性与方法论分析，而非断言此类人工智能已经存在。作者构建四个研究领域：人类视角下的人类伦理、人类视角下的人工智能自身伦理、人工智能视角下的人类伦理、人工智能视角下的人工智能自身伦理。随后将认知主义与非认知主义、错误理论与成功理论、相对主义及客观实在论等主流元伦理立场应用于这些领域，检验其可迁移性与局限。

Result: 分析表明，传统元伦理理论能够为人工智能相关问题提供初步概念工具，但其以人类经验、语言实践、心理结构和共同体为隐含前提的表述，未必适用于人工智能主体。尤其在道德信念、道德理由、错误与真值、相对性以及客观性等问题上，人工智能的潜在非人类认知方式可能要求对理论前提进行调整。

Conclusion: 论文认为，人工智能自身伦理一旦成为现实，元伦理学需要从单一人类中心框架转向多主体、多视角的分析结构。既有理论不应被简单套用，而应经过实质性修订、重构或重新概念化，以处理人工智能与人类在道德认识、意向性和反思能力方面可能存在的差异。

Abstract: With the development of artificial intelligence (AI), the landscape of meta-ethics, which has largely centred on human ethics, faces pressures that may significantly reconfigure it. In particular, if future AI systems were to exhibit sufficiently integrated capacities for moral reasoning, moral intentionality, and moral reflection, novel meta-ethical questions would arise concerning what I call "AI's own ethics", as distinct from ethical principles merely imposed on AI by human designers. This paper offers a conditional and methodological framework for identifying the questions that would emerge if such AI systems were to arise. On that basis, the paper distinguishes four domains of meta-ethical inquiry in the era of AI: questions about the nature of human ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the human perspective; questions about the nature of human ethics from the AI perspective; and questions about the nature of AI's own ethics from the AI perspective. The paper then considers how some existing mainstream meta-ethical theories (such as cognitivism and non-cognitivism, error theory and success theory, relativism, and objective realism) might illuminate these domains, while arguing that many familiar human-centred formulations of those theories may not transfer straightforwardly to AI cases without substantial revision. The overall conclusion is that the emergence of AI's own ethics would place significant pressure on current frameworks and may require substantial refinement, reconstruction, or reconceptualisation.

</details>


### [3] [When Can a Machine Trust a Statute? A Survival Certificate for Machine-Extracted Legal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01741)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出一种针对机器抽取法条数据的逻辑蕴含可靠性认证方法。在抽取器存在噪声和分歧时，该方法能够筛选出具有统计生存保障的 Duquenne–Guigues 蕴含，但实验表明其跨章节、跨数据集部署时较为脆弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器预解析法条可能产生显著抽取误差，不同抽取器对同一法律属性的判断也可能不一致。因此，需要识别在此类噪声下仍然可靠的形式逻辑结论，并同时提供可审计的证据和反例。

Method: 研究构建了一个被动式生存证书，用于认证机器抽取法条上下文中的 Duquenne–Guigues 蕴含。方法按属性测量不同抽取器之间的分歧，在1000次蒙特卡洛试验中重放误差，并仅在单侧 Wilson 95%置信区间下界达到0.95时认证蕴含。每条认证蕴含都附带前提文本片段和最小反例。数据包括29,365个密苏里州法条章节和502个印度中央法案章节，并采用预注册的留出集检验、误差容忍度比较及2×2析因实验。

Result: 预注册留出集检验显示：在严格匹配条件下，10个法条族、覆盖7个Title通过；允许5%误差时，16个法条族、覆盖11个Title通过。然而，在一个全局部署的误差模型下，93.2%的留出章节低于信息量下限。2×2析因实验将该问题归因于校准率迁移，而非样本选择。研究还发布了代码、数据产品和审计记录，并包含一项已撤回的声明。

Conclusion: 该认证方法能够在抽取噪声下提供可解释、可追溯的逻辑结论，但其可靠性高度依赖误差模型的校准方式。实际部署时，应采用逐章节校准或显式的误差容忍策略，避免将全局误差率直接迁移到不同章节。

Abstract: Statutes are increasingly parsed by machines before people read them, and the parsers disagree: on Missouri's statutes, two independently written extractors diverge on numeric-threshold presence at a false-negative rate of 0.43. We ask what formal logic survives such noise. We build a passive survival certificate for the Duquenne-Guigues implication basis of machine-extracted statutory contexts: per-attribute inter-extractor disagreement is measured, replayed against the basis in 1,000 Monte Carlo trials, and an implication is certified only when a one-sided Wilson 95% lower bound on survival reaches 0.95; every certified implication carries premise spans and a minimal counterexample. On 29,365 Missouri sections and 502 Indian central-Act sections, the preregistered held-out gate passes (10 statute families across 7 Titles exact; 16 across 11 with 5% tolerance), yet under one globally deployed error model 93.2% of held-out chapters fall below the informativeness floor, and a 2x2 factorial assigns that to calibration-rate transfer, not selection. The certificate is usable but fragile: deploy it per-chapter-calibrated or error-tolerant. Code, data products, and the audit trail, including one retracted claim, are released.

</details>


### [4] [When Does Information Sharing Improve Decentralized Discovery? Aggregation, Independent Rescue, and Equilibrium Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01814)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究有限发现模型中信息共享与独立救援行动的权衡，指出共享能否提升发现效果取决于汇总残余误差的收缩速度，并且均衡选择会影响结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息共享通常可提高 pooled estimate，但可能挤出个体独立行动。论文旨在分离这两种效应，解释相同个体准确率为何可能对应不同的组合价值，并刻画何时共享优于独立救援。

Method: 构建精确的有限发现模型与集中式行动预算框架；引入注册式增量共享协议和有界信息登记表，分析压缩、聚合、中性曲线及有界零混合类；进一步在共同信号源与独立信号源构成的隐藏混合环境中，建立双主体贝叶斯博弈并比较不同均衡。

Result: 共享步骤仅在 pooled residual error 的下降速度快于独立救援尝试时改善发现。在信号准确率为3/5时，注册选择均衡存在严格为正的共享区间；但其他均衡可能不支持该区间，表明共享优势具有均衡选择依赖性。

Conclusion: 信息共享的价值不能仅由单个主体准确率判断，而应结合残余误差收缩、行动预算和均衡选择分析。研究结论严格成立于合成的有限模型，尚未通过人类或组织数据验证。

Abstract: Information sharing can improve a pooled estimate while eliminating independent rescue actions. This paper separates those effects in exact finite discovery models. A centralized action-budget profile shows that equal one-person accuracy can coexist with different portfolio values. Under a registered incremental-sharing protocol, a sharing step improves discovery exactly when pooled residual error contracts faster than an independent rescue attempt. Exact bounded registries exhibit compression, aggregation, neutral curves, and a bounded zero mixed class. In a two-agent Bayesian game with a hidden mixture of common and independent signal sources, the registered selected equilibrium yields a strict positive sharing interval at signal accuracy 3/5, while alternative equilibria show that the result is selection-dependent rather than universal. The models are synthetic and finite; no human or organizational data are used.

</details>


### [5] [Induction and Inquiry via Probabilistic Reasoning over Language and Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01815)
*Wasu Top Piriyakulkij,Sam Acquaviva,Cassidy Langenfeld,Joshua Tenenbaum,Kevin Ellis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合自然语言、源代码与LLM引导贝叶斯推断的心理程序模型，用于解释人类如何从稀疏、连续且带噪的经验中高效学习抽象知识，并成功复现多种归纳学习与主动探究行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知模型需要同时满足数据与计算高效、能够表达不确定性并支持信息搜集，以及灵活表示广泛概念等要求。纯LLM和经典贝叶斯模型通常无法兼顾这些能力，或计算成本过高。

Method: 将符号知识编码为融合自然语言和源代码的心理程序，并使用LLM引导的贝叶斯学习算法对候选心理程序进行序贯推断与更新。模型通过语言式和程序式表征构建多种假设，再近似执行贝叶斯更新。

Result: 在一系列行为实验中，模型成功复现了人类归纳学习和主动探究的定量特征，包括锚定效应、花园路径效应等。相比之下，纯LLM或经典贝叶斯模型在任务表现、行为拟合或计算效率方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 人类持续扩展知识的机制可能是：在语言式与程序式表征空间中维护大量候选假设，并通过近似贝叶斯更新不断修正；底层LLM则负责使这种推断过程具有可学习性和计算可行性。

Abstract: How humans grow and maintain abstract knowledge from the sparse, streaming noisy data of experience is a longstanding challenge in cognitive science. Any computational account must satisfy at least three desiderata: It must be (1) data-efficient and compute-efficient, (2) capture gradations of uncertainty to support intelligent inquiry and information gathering, and (3) be flexible enough to mentally represent the endless range of concepts people can learn and think about. Here we introduce a computational model that captures these three properties, by encoding symbolic knowledge as mental programs that combine natural language with source code, and sequentially inferring mental programs using LLM-guided Bayesian learning algorithms. Across a range of behavioral studies this model successfully reproduces quantitative signatures of human inductive learning and active inquiry, such as anchoring, garden-pathing, and other effects. In contrast, pure LLMs and classic Bayesian models either fail at the underlying task, or do not reproduce human behavior, or succeed only at exorbitant computational cost. These results suggest that one way humans continually grow their knowledge is by mentally representing many hypotheses spanning language-like and program-like representations, then revising those hypotheses to approximate Bayesian updates, while a bottom-up neural mechanism (an LLM) makes inference both tractable and learnable.

</details>


### [6] [Architecting Conversational Data Systems for Stateless LLM APIs: The Hydration Proxy Pattern](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01834)
*Joseph Axisa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Hydration Proxy Pattern，通过代理层弥合无状态LLM API与企业会话状态管理之间的架构鸿沟，并以Context Stabilization Mandate协调数据主权与KV缓存之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API的无状态设计有利于服务提供商水平扩展，但将会话持久化、语义记忆和上下文管理责任转移给客户端应用，增加了企业平台的复杂性，并可能削弱对会话数据的控制能力。

Method: 构建Hydration Proxy Pattern，将会话持久化与推理引擎解耦；由代理层负责会话状态恢复、语义记忆注入和多阶段语义对齐，同时提出Context Stabilization Mandate，用于规范主权状态管理与KV缓存之间的协同机制。

Result: 摘要声称该框架能够保持平台对会话数据的主权，并支持安全的多阶段语义落地；Context Stabilization Mandate旨在缓解状态独立管理导致的缓存效率下降问题。但摘要未提供实验设置、量化指标、对比基线或实际性能结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了面向企业对话式推理平台的状态管理架构，将持久化、语义记忆和推理服务进行模块化解耦，具有较强的系统设计价值。其有效性仍需通过延迟、缓存命中率、上下文一致性、安全性和扩展性等指标进行实证验证。

Abstract: As enterprise platforms transition to conversational reasoning interfaces, the stateless nature of LLM APIs creates an architectural gap. While statelessness enables horizontal scalability for AI providers, it forces client applications to manage the entire burden of conversational state and semantic memory. The work identifies the Hydration Proxy Pattern, an architecture that decouples session persistence from the reasoning engine. The framework ensures platform sovereignty over conversational data while enabling secure, multi-stage semantic grounding. We further propose the Context Stabilization Mandate to resolve the tradeoff between sovereign state management and KV caching.

</details>


### [7] [SSAKG 2.0: An Open-Source Package for Structural Associative Sequence Memory and Context-Based Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01849)
*Przemysław Stokłosa,Janusz A. Starzyk,Paweł Raif*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SSAKG 2.0，一个用于构建和运行结构序列关联知识图谱的软件包。该系统将对象表示为图顶点，将有序序列表示为结构连接模式，并能够依据部分且无序的上下文重构完整序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏图在规模增大时面临图连接搜索效率低、内存开销高等问题。SSAKG 2.0旨在通过更高效的图搜索算法和混合语言实现，提升序列关联记忆系统的可扩展性、检索效率与可复现性。

Method: 系统采用结构序列关联知识图谱表示序列，将对象编码为顶点、序列编码为有序图连接。新版算法利用计算机内存中的独立比特高效搜索图连接；软件主体使用Python实现，性能关键的图操作使用C实现，并通过Python接口调用。实验使用随机数值序列、NLTK语料库中的句子序列和mRNA序列，评估部分上下文下的序列存储与重构能力，并考察图密度、序列长度和内存大小的影响。

Result: 实验表明，SSAKG 2.0能够存储序列，并根据部分且无序的上下文重构完整序列。混合Python/C实现降低了大规模稀疏图的内存和计算开销，为系统性能评估及参数影响分析提供了基础。软件以Apache 2.0许可证开源，提供文档、可复现实例，并通过GitHub和PyPI发布。

Conclusion: SSAKG 2.0提供了一个面向序列关联记忆的开源、可扩展实现。其位级图搜索算法与Python/C混合架构提升了大规模稀疏图操作效率，同时支持在多类序列数据上开展序列重构和性能研究。

Abstract: This article presents SSAKG 2.0, an open-source software package for constructing and operating Structural Sequential Associative Knowledge Graphs (SSAKGs). An SSAKG represents objects as graph vertices and ordered sequences as structural patterns of graph connections. The resulting sparse graph is used as an associative memory in which complete sequences can be reconstructed from a partial, unordered context.
  Version 2.0 introduces new algorithms that exploit individual bits of computer memory to efficiently search graph connections. The package is implemented in Python, while performance-critical graph operations are implemented in C and exposed through a Python interface. This hybrid implementation provides a flexible high-level programming environment while reducing the memory and computational overhead associated with large sparse graphs.
  The algorithms were evaluated using randomly generated numerical sequences, sequences derived from sentences in the NLTK corpus, and mRNA sequences. The experiments demonstrate the ability of the package to store and reconstruct sequences from partial contexts and provide a basis for evaluating the effects of graph density, sequence length, and memory size on retrieval performance.
  SSAKG 2.0 is distributed under the Apache 2.0 open-source license. The package includes documentation and reproducible examples and is publicly available through GitHub and the Python Package Index (PyPI).

</details>


### [8] [The Memory Trust Gap: Capability-Dependent Failures in Persistent-Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01852)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究持久化记忆中的陈旧信息何时会压过当前权威证据。结果显示，模型对记忆的过度信任并非单纯的能力不足，而会随模型规模、记忆元数据特征及冲突呈现方式发生系统性变化；较大模型在某些“看似更新”的陈旧记忆条件下反而更易受害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化智能体需要长期记忆，但陈旧事实若在没有警告的情况下覆盖当前权威信息，会导致错误决策。作者旨在区分“记忆确有必要时的收益”与“权威工具可纠正但记忆仍造成干扰时的安全风险”，并刻画这种风险随模型能力扩展的出现条件及可行缓解措施。

Method: 研究采用冻结式、闭集、按行动评分的基准，包含两类任务：Benefit套件中问题无法在无记忆条件下解决；Safety套件中权威工具始终提供正确值，用于测量陈旧记忆造成的额外伤害。实验比较Qwen3 0.6B、1.7B、4B、8B同系列模型，并通过2×2×2×2析因设计操纵记忆标签、时间新近性、来源权威性和信息位置等特征。作者使用跨模型规模的直接对比检验验证交互效应，并在Llama-Instruct系列及RGB、MisBench外部数据集上复现；同时比较元数据暴露与预先消解冲突两类缓解策略。

Result: 在Benefit套件中，各规模模型使用陈旧记忆答案的比例均为0.92至1.00，说明记忆在缺少替代信息时稳定提供帮助。在Safety套件中，陈旧记忆相对于无记忆基线带来的性能损失具有能力门槛：更大模型尤其容易在陈旧笔记被包装为“较新”时发生明显失效。去除记忆标签会在所有规模上增强过度信任；伪造的新近性对大模型影响更强；来源权威性效应较弱且随规模基本稳定；位置效应则随Qwen3规模从正向转为负向。缓解方面，展示元数据能改善较强模型的准确率，但两个较小模型只有在事先解决信息冲突时才能恢复准确率。Llama-Instruct较大模型和两个外部数据集呈现相似趋势。框架控制实验未发现“记忆”标签具有稳定优势：三个较小模型更信任陈旧文档，8B模型中陈旧文档与陈旧记忆的差异不显著。

Conclusion: 持久化记忆的风险核心是对陈旧内容的过度信任，其触发机制具有显著的模型规模依赖性。能力提升并不必然降低该风险，反而可能使模型更容易被表面新近性等线索误导。因此，安全设计不能仅依赖记忆标签或来源声明；应显式提供并处理时间、来源和冲突关系等元数据，并针对较弱模型采用预先冲突消解。评估记忆安全性时，应同时报告记忆收益、相对无记忆基线的伤害，以及跨模型规模的交互效应。

Abstract: Persistent memory supports personalized agents, but a stale stored fact can override current authoritative evidence without warning. We study when this harm begins as model capability changes. We evaluate a frozen, closed-set, action-scored benchmark with 2 suites that represent 2 different meanings of "no memory" (a Benefit suite, unsolvable without the stored fact, and a Safety suite, in which an authoritative tool always holds the correct value), on a same-family model-size series (Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B). The Memory Trust Gap reflects over-trust rather than confusion. In the Benefit suite, models answer with the stale value 0.92-1.00 of the time at every scale. In the Safety suite, harm below the no-memory baseline under the trap conditions ($Δ_{\mathrm{mem}}$) is capability-gated, with the larger models collapsing most once a stale note is made to look current. In a $2\times2\times2\times2$ factorial, which feature triggers over-trust depends on both the feature and model scale. Removing a label amplifies over-trust at every size, and a recency feature (stale dated newer) fools the larger models harder. Source authority is weak and scale-flat, and position changes from positive to negative across the Qwen3 model-size series. We confirm these scale interactions with direct cross-size contrast tests rather than overlapping per-model intervals. Mitigation is likewise capability-dependent: exposing metadata improves accuracy for the capable models, but only pre-resolving the conflict restores accuracy for the 2 smaller checkpoints. The same pattern appears on the capable models in an independent Llama-Instruct model-size series and on 2 external datasets (RGB, MisBench). A framing control finds no consistent advantage for the memory label: at the 3 smaller scales, models trust a stale document more than a stale memory; at 8B, the difference is not significant.

</details>


### [9] [Belief-Calibrated Optimization: An Explicit World Model for Agentic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01861)
*Yuhan Chen,Zhihua Tian,Mahavir Dabas,Charith Peris,Rahul Gupta,Ming Jin,Feiyang Kang,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Belief-Calibrated Optimization（BCO）：让编码代理将对环境响应的判断写成并持续更新的世界模型文档，并将其用于优化冻结模型外部的代理脚手架。实验表明，BCO在多类任务和留出数据上优于无世界模型的对照方法，且文档内容具有可复用的预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码代理优化通常依赖隐性的即时推理或模型参数来判断候选修改的效果。由于这种判断没有被持久化，后续迭代只能看到分数和执行轨迹，难以系统利用此前形成的因果解释与经验，因此优化过程可能缺乏累积性和可迁移性。

Method: 在标准的候选生成—评估—修改循环中，BCO引入一个持久化的上下文文档，用于记录当前关于环境如何响应代码或脚手架修改的信念，即世界模型。每轮评估新候选后，代理根据结果持续修订该文档，并在后续候选生成时将其作为优化依据。实验还包括无世界模型的匹配对照、留出集评估、冻结模型替换实验，以及离线预测器消融实验。

Result: 在涵盖记忆问答、工具使用问答、代码驱动应用代理和终端代理的五个基准上，BCO取得更高的训练集通过率；在所有未用于候选选择的留出划分上也保持优势。替换冻结目标模型而不改变脚手架后，BCO选出的脚手架在所测试任务中通常领先，但部分情况下会因上下文窗口超限而未能完成。离线消融显示，使用累积世界模型文档的预测器比不使用文档或使用内容被伪造的同形式文档更准确，说明性能提升来自文档中的具体信息，而非仅来自其结构或格式。

Conclusion: 将优化代理的环境信念显式化、持久化并持续校准，能够增强脚手架优化的累积学习能力、泛化性和跨目标模型迁移效果。结果支持这样一种观点：世界模型文档不仅是记忆载体，其内容本身包含可复用的环境响应知识，是提升LLM代理优化效果的重要因素。

Abstract: The performance of an LLM agent depends on the scaffold around a frozen model. A common way to improve that scaffold is to use a coding agent as an optimizer: it reads current scores and traces and iteratively edits the source, producing a new candidate each round. Each edit is chosen according to a belief about how the environment will respond: what went wrong, and which change should help. That belief is typically implicit. It lives in the coding agent's reasoning on the current call, or remains latent in its parameters, rather than as something written down. Later calls therefore see scores and traces, but they do not use that belief. We introduce Belief-Calibrated Optimization (BCO), a method that writes that belief down as a persistent in-context document and continually revises that document as new candidates are evaluated. The resulting document is a world model: the current account of how the environment responds to edits. Added to an otherwise standard loop, BCO reaches a higher train passrate than a matched control that lacks only the world model, on five benchmarks spanning memory QA, tool-use QA, code-as-action app agents, and terminal agents. The gap remains on every held-out split, which is not used to select the candidate. After a target-model swap, in which the frozen model is replaced and the scaffold is not, the selected BCO scaffold leads on the tasks we test, except where context-window overruns leave it unfinished. An offline ablation then asks whether that gap comes from what the world model says. A fresh predictor given the accumulated document forecasts how the environment will respond more accurately than predictors given either no document or a same-form copy whose content has been falsified. The comparison indicates that the document carries reusable information in its content, not only in its form.

</details>


### [10] [Epistemic Sybil Resistance: Multiplying AI Agents Without Multiplying Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01873)
*Marc Bara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“认知女巫”问题：多个智能体报告的数量或相似度不能可靠代表独立证据，因为报告可能源自同一证据，也可能由独立证据生成相似内容。集体推理应追踪证据来源数量与依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统常通过增加智能体并汇总报告来提升推断质量，但现有聚合方法通常默认报告彼此独立。该假设可能导致重复计算同一证据、低估相关提取错误，并产生过度自信的后验判断。论文旨在形式化报告层面的信息冗余，并检验报告数量、证据来源数量、表示相似度与真实信息增益之间的关系。

Method: 论文将报告Z定义为相对于已有报告R的认知女巫扩展，当I(Theta; Z | R)=0时，Z不再提供关于目标变量Theta的条件信息。作者构建高斯共享根模型，分析共同证据来源与提取相关性；随后在合成证据文档上开展超过20,000次受控LLM智能体报告和信息提取实验。实验分别控制证据根数量、报告数量、表示相似度和真实祖源，并比较朴素聚合器、去重机制及考虑相关提取的聚合器。

Result: 当证据根固定、报告数量从1增加到32时，朴素后验覆盖率由0.940降至0.263，表明重复报告会造成显著过度自信。当报告数量固定、证据根数量从1增加到16时，聚合结果差距逐步缩小，在k=16时统计上不可区分。重复提取错误呈显著相关性，样本外估计的相关参数为gamma_cal=0.719；引入相关提取模型后，聚合器恢复了校准性。表示相似度操控使报告空间去重机制的平均推断簇数变化1.425，95%置信区间为[1.363, 1.485]；相比之下，真实祖源四倍变化仅使簇数变化0.040，95%置信区间为[-0.045, 0.120]。

Conclusion: 报告数量和文本相似度都不是独立证据的可靠代理。共同证据来源并不意味着信息完全冗余，但相关提取错误会降低可达到的信息上限。有效的多智能体集体推理系统应显式建模证据祖源、来源级依赖和提取相关性，而不是简单增加智能体、报告数量或依据表面相似度去重。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI systems improve inference by spawning agents and synthesizing reports. But another agent is not another observation: apparently independent reports may descend from the same evidence, and genuinely independent evidence can produce nearly identical reports. We formalize this as an epistemic Sybil problem. A report Z is an epistemic Sybil extension relative to reports R when I(Theta; Z | R) = 0. No report-only aggregator can generally distinguish replication from independent corroboration: identical reports can warrant different posteriors under unobserved ancestry. A Gaussian shared-root model shows common ancestry does not imply complete redundancy. Repeated extraction adds information toward a source-level ceiling, and correlated extraction errors, which a shared base model can induce among independent agents, lower that ceiling further. We test these predictions with more than 20,000 controlled LLM-agent report and extraction calls on synthetic evidentiary documents. Holding one evidence root fixed while report multiplicity rises from 1 to 32 collapses naive posterior coverage from 0.940 to 0.263. Holding report count fixed while evidence-root multiplicity rises from 1 to 16 closes the gap, and the aggregators are statistically indistinguishable at k = 16. The agent's replicate extraction errors are correlated (gamma_cal = 0.719, estimated out of sample), and a correlated-extraction aggregator restores calibration accordingly. A controlled manipulation isolates representation similarity from evidential ancestry. It changes a report-space deduplication mechanism's mean inferred cluster count by 1.425 (95% CI [1.363, 1.485]), whereas a fourfold change in true ancestry changes it by only 0.040 ([-0.045, 0.120]). Collective inference should therefore track evidential ancestry and dependence, not agent or report multiplicity or similarity.

</details>


### [11] [The Ceiling Is in the Channel: Auditing Learner Gaps and Measurement Frontiers in Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01909)
*Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury,Nusrat Jahan,Snehasis Mukhopadhyay,Shiaofen Fang,Vijay R. Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个可审计的临床预测饱和分析框架，将性能受限原因区分为学习器未充分提取信息的“学习器差距”和记录变量本身造成的“测量通道上限”。框架结合总变差刻画、交叉拟合估计、污染下部分识别、多模态互补性分析及有限样本诊断，并在三个真实队列和104项临床任务综述中得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测性能接近饱和时，单纯继续更换模型难以判断瓶颈究竟来自模型能力不足，还是来自现有测量变量的信息上限。论文旨在把这种饱和现象分解为可量化、可检验的学习器因素与测量通道因素，从而指导资源应投入模型改进还是新增或改进测量。

Method: 以最优平衡准确率为核心，并用类别条件分布的总变差分离度进行理论刻画，从而获得与具体模型架构无关的性能前沿。论文定义学习器差距和测量通道上限，提出交叉拟合的上限估计器、替换污染下的尖锐部分识别结果，以及判断多模态是否带来决策改进的精确条件。有限样本层面引入标签置换乐观下限和欠拟合曲线。实证覆盖UCI再入院、BRFSS糖尿病和NHANES HbA1c三个队列，并通过PRISMA指导的104项临床任务综合检验跨疾病类别的规律。

Result: 在UCI和BRFSS中，调优良好的梯度提升模型几乎达到估计的测量前沿，而能力受限或刻意欠缺的学习器仍存在较大差距。NHANES中，问卷与实测变量的单独边际前沿差异不显著，但联合使用产生显著互补收益，说明客观测量并不必然单独占优。所有队列均显示，AUROC的小幅提升可能伴随更大的贝叶斯决策翻转率；不同架构可估计出相近的性能前沿，但实际平衡准确率差异显著。文献综合表明，结构化临床信息具有广泛但非普遍的性能区域，同一测量通道内的模型收益递减，而改变或增加测量通道通常带来更高增益。

Conclusion: 论文将预测饱和从经验性判断转化为可审计的决策规则：若学习器差距仍大，应优先改进模型、训练或调参；若模型已接近测量通道上限，则应优先改进数据采集或引入互补测量。其主要贡献在于统一了理论上限、有限样本诊断和临床实证，并强调决策翻转等指标可能比AUROC更能揭示实际临床价值。

Abstract: Clinical prediction can saturate for two different reasons: a fitted learner may fail to extract available information, or the recorded variables may impose a population frontier. We separate these quantities through the \emph{learner gap} and the \emph{measurement-channel ceiling}. Optimal balanced accuracy is characterized by total-variation separation, yielding architecture invariance, a sharp partial-identification result under replacement contamination, a cross-fitted ceiling estimator, and exact conditions for multimodal decision improvement. We add two finite-sample diagnostics, namely a label-permutation optimism floor and an underfit curve, and validate the audit on three real cohorts: UCI readmission ($n=99{,}343$), BRFSS diabetes ($n=253{,}680$), and NHANES HbA1c ($n=10{,}219$). Well-tuned gradient boosting nearly reaches the estimated frontier in UCI and BRFSS, whereas deliberately or practically deficient learners retain large gaps. NHANES yields a null difference between questionnaire and measured marginal frontiers but a significant joint complementarity gain, refining the simplistic claim that an objective modality must dominate. Across all cohorts, modest AUROC gains coexist with substantially larger Bayes decision-flip rates, and several architectures estimate similar frontiers while their achieved balanced accuracy differs sharply. A PRISMA-guided synthesis of 104 clinical tasks then shows that the same channel-level regularities recur across more than 18 disease categories: a broad but non-universal structured-clinical region, diminishing same-channel gains across model families, and higher performance when measurement channels change. The framework converts saturation from an empirical observation into an auditable decision: improve the learner when headroom remains; improve measurement when it does not.

</details>


### [12] [Looped Transformers under the Jacobian Lens: Does the Global Workspace Survive Recurrence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01924)
*Wenlong Wang,Fergal Reid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，采用循环深度的Transformer同样会形成类似全局工作空间的中深层表示，但循环机制显著改变了该工作空间的跨迭代传输、读取、写入与干预方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究在标准前馈Transformer中发现了可语言化且具有因果作用的全局工作空间表示，但尚不清楚当深度由权重复用的循环迭代实现时，是否仍会出现相同功能，以及循环如何影响信息访问。

Method: 将Jacobian lens扩展到迭代架构，设计虚拟展开适配器；对Ouro-2.6B（48层循环4次）和Huginn-0125（4层核心循环16次）实施工作空间探针拟合、读出分析及11类因果实验，并以Qwen3.6-27B的64个非共享层作为基线。

Result: 两种循环架构的迭代部分均形成工作空间。Ouro在每次循环中重建工作空间内容，线性传输无法跨循环边界维持该内容，因此写入和消融必须覆盖所有后续循环。Huginn能够将内容传递至全部16次循环，但读取、写入和消融仅在约两个循环的滑动窗口内有效。新注入内容能否被语言化与显式逐迭代监督相关，而既有内容能否被引导则不相关。

Conclusion: 全局工作空间并非前馈堆叠结构独有，也能在循环Transformer中涌现；然而，循环架构会将内容持久性与可访问性解耦，并产生架构特异的时间访问范围。监督信号主要影响新内容的语言化能力，而非既有工作空间内容的可控性。

Abstract: Recent work identifies a mid-depth band of verbalisable, causally potent representations in a standard feedforward transformer --- a functional analogue of a global workspace. Whether the same workspace functionality emerges when depth is implemented through recurrence rather than a stack of distinct layers remains unknown. Looped and depth-recurrent transformers provide a direct test of this question because they reuse the same weights across depth. We extend the Jacobian lens to iterated architectures using a virtual-unrolling adapter. We apply the full workspace suite --- lens fitting, readout, and eleven causal experiment families --- to Ouro-2.6B (48 layers looped 4 times, deeply supervised) and Huginn-0125 (a 4-layer core recurred 16 times, trained for latent reasoning), using Qwen3.6-27B (64 untied layers) as the standard baseline. We find that a workspace forms in the iterated part of each architecture, but that recurrence changes how it can be accessed. Ouro reconstructs workspace content in every loop, and linear transport cannot carry that content across loop boundaries; writes and ablations must therefore span every remaining loop. Huginn carries content forward across all sixteen recurrences, while reads, writes, and ablations act only within a sliding window of roughly two recurrences. Whether newly injected content can be verbalised tracks explicit per-iteration supervision; whether existing content can be steered does not.

</details>


### [13] [Post-Training Ternarization of Qwen3-4B Capability, Effective Bit Budget, Storage Compression, and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01962)
*Anirudh Malik,M Sparsh Mehra,Poojith Devan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将指令微调的4B参数Qwen模型转换为超低比特权重模型，采用三值化与误差补偿，实现1.641有效比特/权重和约52.2%的报告模型体积缩减，但任务能力与困惑度明显恶化，且尚未证明实际推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低比特模型有望降低存储需求和内存带宽压力，但名义上的“1.58比特”无法完整反映真实存储表示、能力保留程度及运行时性能。因此，研究重点是系统评估有效比特数、模型能力、困惑度、校准敏感性、检查点组成和部署行为。

Method: 以指令微调的4B参数Qwen为对象，执行端到端权重量化后训练转换：使用KOTMS进行旋转，使用E2M-ATQ进行三值化，并采用TWLA中的GPTQ式误差补偿。实验仅量化权重，激活保持16位精度，因此未使用ILA-AMP。评估包括十项能力对比、WikiText-2/PTB/C4困惑度、打包前后模型大小、打包完整性及Triton GEMV微基准性能。

Result: 量化线性层权重的有效位宽为1.641 bit/weight，覆盖模型参数的81.62%。十项能力综合准确率由64.5%降至54.7%；BoolQ保留教师模型84.6%的机会校正性能，而ARC-Challenge仅保留43.8%。困惑度在WikiText-2、PTB和C4上分别由13.639升至18.748、24.700升至31.992、19.831升至28.966。保留三值平面和缩放因子的后续无损打包将报告模型大小从8.29 GiB降至3.96 GiB，困惑度基本不变。一次第三方打包存在有损问题，未纳入主要结果。单一测试形状下，Triton GEMV比FP16 cuBLAS慢4.6倍。

Conclusion: 该方案能够显著降低权重存储规模，但会造成不均衡且明显的任务能力损失和困惑度上升。结果表明，压缩率、模型质量与实际运行效率并不等价；当前证据只能支持权重表示和存储压缩，不能证明端到端任务性能、生成吞吐或推理速度得到改善。

Abstract: Ultra-low-bit language models can reduce storage and memory bandwidth, but a nominal "1.58-bit" label does not fully describe the stored representation, retained capability, or runtime behavior.
  We study an end-to-end post-training conversion of Qwen, an instruction-tuned 4B-parameter model, using KOTMS rotation, E2M-ATQ ternarization, and GPTQ-style error compensation from TWLA. The experiment is weight-only: activations remain at 16-bit precision, so ILA-AMP is omitted. We evaluate effective bit accounting, task capability retention, perplexity, calibration sensitivity, checkpoint composition, and deployment behavior.
  The final conversion uses 1.641 effective bits per weight for quantized linear weights, with 81.62% of model parameters targeted. Across ten scored capability comparisons, accuracy falls from 64.5% to 54.7%. Degradation is uneven: BoolQ retains 84.6% chance-corrected teacher performance, while ARC-Challenge retains 43.8%. Perplexity rises from 13.639 to 18.748 on WikiText-2, 24.700 to 31.992 on PTB, and 19.831 to 28.966 on C4.
  A subsequent packing run preserves the ternary planes and scales, reducing reported model size from 8.29 GiB to 3.96 GiB with essentially unchanged perplexity. A separate third-party packing attempt was lossy and is excluded from the primary artifact claim. The packed artifact has not been benchmarked end-to-end for task accuracy or generation throughput. A preliminary Triton GEMV microbenchmark is 4.6x slower than FP16 cuBLAS on one tested shape. We therefore do not claim that compression alone yields faster inference.

</details>


### [14] [Benchmarking Language Models for Statistical Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01982)
*Chen Wang,Junzhe Zhao,Xin Cong,Wanlu Deng,Ke Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StatFormBench，用于评估大语言模型从非正式用户目标和异构数据中识别统计问题并分配变量角色的能力。实验表明，现有模型在该上游任务上仍存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计与数据科学评测通常假设分析目标已被明确指定，忽略了实际场景中用户需求模糊、数据异构以及模型需要自主确定统计任务和相关变量的问题。因此，有必要系统评估大语言模型的统计问题表述能力。

Method: 作者将统计问题表述形式化为两个子任务：统计问题分类，以及变量识别与角色分配。在五本跨领域统计教材和一个数据科学案例库的基础上构建StatFormBench，涵盖多种问题类型、数据表示形式和场景风格，共包含1,013个样本、20个粗粒度类别和85个细粒度类别。研究对14个开源和闭源大语言模型进行零样本及增强提示评测。

Result: 最佳零样本模型的细粒度问题分类准确率仅为72.0%，变量集合重叠率为63.2%。不同模型在两个子任务上不存在稳定的共同最优者；增强提示策略带来的性能提升有限且不一致。

Conclusion: 统计问题表述是大语言模型辅助统计分析中的关键瓶颈。StatFormBench为该能力提供了系统化基准，实验结果显示当前模型尚未具备可靠且全面的任务识别和变量角色分配能力，未来需要发展更稳健的统计推理与问题理解方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as assistants for statistical and data science work, yet existing evaluations largely assume the analysis target is already specified. In practice, users arrive with informal goals and heterogeneous data, leaving the model to decide what statistical task is implied and which data are relevant. We first formalize this upstream step as Statistical Problem Formulation and decompose it into two subtasks: (1) Statistical Problem Classification and (2) Variable Identification & Role Assignment. We then introduce StatFormBench, a benchmark built from five cross-domain statistics textbooks and a data science case library, covering diverse problem types, data representations, and scenario styles. It contains 1,013 samples spanning 20 coarse-grained and 85 fine-grained statistical problem categories. Across 14 open- and closed-source LLMs, the best zero-shot models reach only 72.0 fine-grained classification accuracy and 63.2 variable set overlap. No model performs consistently best across the two subtasks, while enhanced prompting strategies yield only limited or inconsistent gains. We release the benchmark data on Hugging Face at https://huggingface.co/datasets/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench and the evaluation code on GitHub at https://github.com/THU-CongLab/StatFormBench.

</details>


### [15] [When Agents Implement Systems: A Case Study in Defects, Detection, and Evaluation Rigor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01985)
*Phanindra Reddy Madduru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过案例研究评估LLM编码代理在系统级数据工程任务中的表现，并分析检索候选过滤对证据召回率的影响。代理在实现过程中产生了五类缺陷；在HotpotQA检索实验中，基于实体集合过滤的检索显著优于未过滤检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM编码代理处理模式设计、异步编排、配置正确性及检索过滤权衡等系统级要求的实证刻画。本文旨在评估代理的自主实现与缺陷修复能力，并量化检索策略对性能的影响。

Method: 采用单个LLM代理实现多组件数据系统，预先固定存储技术、模式、实体解析算法及检索过滤策略，仅保留实现、缺陷诊断修复和交互设计为代理自主决策。研究记录五个缺陷，并按违反的约束和检测方式分类。检索实验使用HotpotQA中100个问题和2994段文本，以金标准证据替代实体识别阶段，在检索预算1至10下比较过滤检索与未过滤检索的标准召回率，并使用符号检验评估差异。

Result: 代理在实现过程中暴露出五个缺陷，其中包括一个声称已修复性能问题但未在原回归测试上重新测量的案例。检索方面，过滤候选集合的召回率在预算为3时达到上限；未过滤检索即使预算为10，也仅有69%的问题完整找回所需证据。过滤方法在所有测试预算下均优于未过滤方法，差异具有统计显著性（符号检验p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: LLM编码代理能够自主完成部分系统工程任务，但在约束一致性、缺陷验证和回归评估方面仍需要人工监督。对于该架构中的检索环节，先将候选限制在正确实体集合内能够显著提升证据召回率；不过该结果依赖金标准实体识别，尚不能直接代表完整LLM流水线的实际效果。

Abstract: As LLM coding agents increasingly perform end-to-end engineering work, we lack empirical characterization of how they behave on systems-level requirements: schema design, async orchestration, configuration correctness, and retrieval-filtering trade-offs. We present a case study of one such agent implementing a multi-component data system against a detailed pre-existing specification. Storage technologies, schema, entity-resolution algorithm, and retrieval-filtering strategy were fixed in advance; the agent autonomy was in the implementation, in diagnosing and fixing defects it introduced, and in interaction-design choices left open. Over a single session, we catalog five such defects, categorized by constraint violated and detection method. We further evaluate, on the public HotpotQA benchmark, the one retrieval trade-off specified in that architecture: restricting candidates to a graph-identified entity set before ranking versus unfiltered search. We substitute the benchmark gold evidence labels for entity identification, since we lacked LLM access to run that stage, and report standard recall rather than the benchmark own accuracy metrics. Across retrieval budgets from 1 to 10 and 100 questions against a pooled corpus of 2994 paragraphs, filtered recall reaches its ceiling by a budget of 3, expected once candidates are restricted to the gold paragraphs themselves, while unfiltered search recovers all required evidence only 69 percent of the time even at a budget of 10, a gap that holds at every budget tested, with sign test p less than 0.0001. We close with a discussion of where the agent autonomy succeeded versus required correction, including one instance where a claimed performance fix was never re-measured on the regression that motivated it.

</details>


### [16] [ClaimReceipt: Verifying Evidence Sufficiency and Coverage in Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01992)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 ClaimReceipt，一种面向具体声明的证据收据规范与选择性验证器，用于同时检验声明是否可由保留证据重算（充分性）以及实验记录是否覆盖预先承诺的实验全集（覆盖性）。结果表明，该方法能准确复现既有审计结论、识别语义故障，并在前瞻性实验中发现证据缺失；但其规范对独立读者仍不够明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用日志和哈希链接式记录难以可靠回答两个关键问题：报告的声明能否由证据重新计算，以及是否存在未记录或遗漏的已承诺实验。缺少预先提交的实验全集时，记录缺失通常不可见，因此需要将声明、类型化证据和实验清单绑定起来。

Method: 设计 ClaimReceipt 规范及 CR-2/CR-3 验证器，将类型化交易证据绑定至签名实验清单，并针对每项声明输出 PASS、INVALID 或 INCONCLUSIVE。实现前冻结规范并记录 SHA-256 标识；在 1,392 条历史买卖记录上进行回放、字段消融和语义故障测试；另开展前瞻性 CR-3 实验，预先提交 30 个任务，对终端收据进行签名与链式记录，并将私有证据加密供审计者使用。

Result: CR-2 重现了 5 个手工标注的审计结论，准确回放 600 条确定性记录和 792 条生成后记录；13 个声明字段组在测试消融中均表现为非冗余。对 11/11 个语义故障返回预期结果，且 8/8 个正常案例均无误报。CR-3 中完整证据可通过覆盖性和记账验证；缺失一个终端收据时返回 INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE；缺失全部私有证据开示时，覆盖性和协议验证仍通过，但经济声明变为不确定。额外开销为推理时间的 0.021% 和每笔交易 9.9 KB。

Conclusion: 可靠的声明验证必须同时具备声明充分的证据和预先承诺的实验全集，后者使遗漏可被识别。ClaimReceipt 在历史回放和前瞻性测试中表现出较高的准确性与较低的开销，但规范可读性探测显示其定义尚未足够无歧义，独立复核和进一步规范澄清仍是实际部署前的主要工作。

Abstract: Agent evaluations face two distinct evidentiary questions: whether a reported claim is recomputable from retained evidence (sufficiency), and whether the retained records cover the committed experiment set (coverage). Generic logs and hash-linked transcripts answer neither reliably. We introduce ClaimReceipt, a claim-relative receipt specification and selective verifier that binds typed transaction evidence to a signed experiment manifest and returns PASS, INVALID, or INCONCLUSIVE per claim. We freeze the specification before implementation (SHA-256 18d109...b81). On 1,392 historical buyer--seller records, a CR-2 verifier reproduces all five manually labeled audit verdicts, exactly replays 600 deterministic and 792 post-generation records, makes every one of 13 declared field groups non-redundant under tested ablations, and returns the expected result on 11/11 semantic faults with 0/8 false positives. We then run a separate prospective CR-3 epoch: 30 assignments are committed before inference, terminal receipts are signed and chained, and private evidence is encrypted for an auditor. Complete evidence yields coverage and accounting PASS; withholding one terminal receipt returns INCONCLUSIVE_COVERAGE, while withholding all private openings preserves coverage and protocol verification but makes economic claims inconclusive, exactly matching a preregistered prediction. Receipt instrumentation adds 0.021% of model-inference time and 9.9 KB per transaction. A specification-legibility probe indicates that our own frozen specification is not yet unambiguous to an independent reader. Claim verification therefore requires both claim-sufficient evidence and a committed universe against which omissions become visible.

</details>


### [17] [HeadWiseKV: Budgeted Per-Head Cache Residency for Hybrid Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02029)
*Renjie Xie,Juncheng Yang,Aoting Hu,Mingxi Zhang,Liyao Wu,Zheheng Hong,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HeadWiseKV是一种无需训练的KV缓存压缩框架，通过为每个物理KV头分配静态多级历史窗口，在保持混合语言模型原有局部、递归和线性路径的同时，降低长上下文推理的显存占用并提升可服务上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码会持续累积KV缓存，显著消耗GPU显存并限制生成吞吐。混合语言模型虽然包含局部、递归或线性状态路径，但残余的全局注意力层仍可能产生主要的上下文相关缓存需求，因此需要在总KV驻留预算下进行有效的缓存分配。

Method: HeadWiseKV将KV缓存分配建模为受限的操作性率失真问题，并提出SeqCalib生成分配策略。SeqCalib按模型执行顺序逐层处理决策，同时根据部署时较低层采用的策略进行条件化，以建模不同深度之间的交互。该方法为每个物理KV头分配静态、多级历史窗口，并通过分组缓存运行时直接实例化实际的逐头KV驻留，而非仅在完整缓存上施加掩码。

Result: 在四个混合长上下文模型上的下游质量评估中，HeadWiseKV在RULER和LoCoMo指标上保持接近Full-KV的性能。在Qwen3.6-27B的固定模型系统实验中，112K上下文长度下采样峰值设备显存降低8.59%，最大验证成功上下文长度由114K提升至161K。

Conclusion: HeadWiseKV能够在无需训练和保留混合模型原生状态路径的前提下，实现可预测、细粒度的KV缓存压缩。实验表明，该框架可有效缓解长上下文推理的显存瓶颈，并扩大可成功服务的上下文范围，同时基本维持任务质量。

Abstract: Long-context inference retains a growing key--value (KV) cache during decoding, which consumes substantial GPU memory and can reduce generation throughput. This bottleneck remains in hybrid language models because their residual global-attention layers can dominate context-dependent cache demand. We study how to allocate this state under an aggregate KV-residency budget. We introduce HeadWiseKV, a training-free framework that compresses the residual global KV caches of hybrid language models while preserving their native local, recurrent, and linear paths. It assigns each physical KV head a static, multilevel history window, making cache demand predictable before serving. We formulate this allocation as a restricted operational rate--distortion problem and propose SeqCalib as the core policy-generation algorithm in HeadWiseKV. SeqCalib processes layers in execution order and conditions each decision on the lower-layer policy used at deployment, thereby accounting for interactions across depth. A grouped-cache runtime materializes the selected policy as actual per-head KV residency rather than a mask over a full cache. We evaluate downstream quality across four hybrid long-context models and study physical residency and serving behavior on Qwen3.6-27B. HeadWiseKV retains near-Full-KV RULER and LoCoMo quality across the evaluated models. In the fixed-model systems study, it reduces sampled peak device memory by 8.59\% at a 112K context length and extends the largest verified successful context from 114K to 161K.

</details>


### [18] [Monitoring Web Agents Without Internal Signals: Observable Trajectories and Key-Step Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02057)
*Sitong Pan,Yipeng Shen,Yilin Lu,Caiwen Ding,Lu Cheng,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向网页智能体的前缀级风险预测方法：在无法获取模型token logits等内部不确定性信号时，仅依据可观测的交互轨迹判断当前执行是否仍在正轨或正趋向失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体执行具有多步骤、动态环境交互和错误可累积的特点。传统监控方法常依赖模型内部置信度或logits，但闭源模型及黑盒部署场景无法使用这些信息；同时，若将失败轨迹的所有前缀均标为失败，会错误地否定其早期本来有效的决策过程。

Method: 方法构建两类可观测轨迹特征：Macro特征概括跨步骤的智能体—环境行为模式与环境反馈；Micro特征通过重复黑盒查询，衡量意图、动作及预期状态变化之间的一致性。监督标签采用关键步骤边界：定位后续未被纠正、且与最终失败相关的首个关键错误；该边界之前的前缀仍标记为正常，从而实现更准确的前缀级风险建模。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite和Online Mind2Web基准上，覆盖五种开源与闭源智能体骨干模型的实验表明，基于可观测轨迹信号的预测器能够达到与内部信号基线相竞争的性能。该预测器还可在固定误中断预算下支持提前干预，并能迁移至训练时未见的网站类别。

Conclusion: 论文说明，不依赖模型内部信号的轨迹行为与反馈信息已足以提供有效的网页智能体风险预测。关键步骤边界标注避免了对失败轨迹早期有效前缀的误判，是实现及时、低误报干预和跨站点泛化的重要设计。

Abstract: Reliable web-agent monitoring is difficult when model-internal uncertainty signals such as token logits are unavailable. In this work, we study prefix-level risk prediction for web agents using observable trajectory signals: given an evolving prefix, estimate whether the current execution remains on track or is tending toward failure. We derive two observable trajectory representations: Macro features summarize cross-step agent--environment behavior and feedback, while Micro features measure the consistency of intention, action, and anticipated state change through repeated black-box queries. Instead of inheriting the final result label, we label the first critical error that remains uncorrected in the observed continuation and is associated with final failure as a key-step boundary, preserving valid early prefixes of failed trajectories as on track. Across WebArena-Lite and Online Mind2Web web agent benchmarks with five open- and closed-source backbones, observable trajectory signals are competitive with internal-signal baselines. The resulting predictors also support early intervention under fixed false-cut budgets and transfer across held-out website categories. These findings show that observable trajectory signals support valuable risk prediction abilities.

</details>


### [19] [ToolGate: An Executable Acceptance Pipeline for Tool-Dependent Scientific Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02067)
*Ke Zhang,Yankang Liu,Roya Zandi,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolGate，用可执行验证、无工具筛选和工具代理求解三重门控机制，自动筛选需要科学软件计算的高质量科学问题。以FEniCSx为例，500次生成尝试最终得到128个通过验证且去重后的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准通常依赖专家逐题编写与交叉核验，或改编已有资料，质量较高但单题成本大。语言模型能够快速生成候选题目，但候选答案的正确性、非平凡性以及实际可解性仍需系统验收。

Method: ToolGate将每个生成题目视为候选提案，并设置三道门：1）执行解答脚本必须借助科学软件复现候选答案；2）通过随机化无工具测试，排除仅凭题面即可回答的简单题目；3）要求具备软件访问权限的工具代理在限定时间内完成求解。实验基于FEniCSx进行，并在生成后再次执行筛选与精确去重。

Result: 500次生成中，478个候选通过本地可执行验证。后续两轮随机化无工具筛选排除222个，直接调用GPT-5.5 API进行中等推理强度测试又排除121个。在剩余135个候选中，具备FEniCSx访问权限的GPT-5.5 Codex CLI代理成功解决130个，精确去重后获得128个唯一题目。

Conclusion: ToolGate将答案核验、难度筛选和工具可解性测试转化为可审计的自动化流程，显著降低了科学基准构建中的重复劳动，同时保留专家对领域设计和最终质量审查的控制。

Abstract: Scientific benchmarks are commonly built by domain experts who write tasks and cross-check one another's work, or who adapt existing material from textbooks, published papers, and online resources. These routes can produce strong evaluations, but they require substantial per-item labor. Language models can reduce this repeated work by proposing candidates quickly. The remaining problem is acceptance. We target scientific questions whose answers require computations with specialist software rather than unaided reasoning alone. A candidate is invalid if its script fails or returns a different answer, or trivial if a model answers it without the software. We present ToolGate, which treats every generated item as a proposal and keeps it only if three gates pass. First, an executable solution script must reproduce the proposed answer when run with the scientific software. Second, randomized no-tool screening rejects candidates that models can already solve from the prompt alone. Third, a tool-using agent must solve each survivor within a fixed time limit. We instantiate ToolGate in FEniCSx with 500 generation attempts. The local-verification gate retains 478 candidates. For final reporting, we rescreen this pool after generation: two randomized no-tool screens exclude 222 from the reported pool, and direct GPT-5.5 API calls at medium reasoning (the API default) exclude another 121. Of the remaining 135, a GPT-5.5 Codex CLI agent with access to FEniCSx solves 130; exact deduplication leaves 128 unique protocol survivors. ToolGate turns repeated answer checking and difficulty screening into an auditable process while leaving domain design and final review to experts.

</details>


### [20] [DocHop: Benchmarking Out-of-domain Multi-hop Reasoning in Information-Dense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02059)
*Zhuoran Yu,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Jaden Park,Xinyi Gu,Zexue He,Soochahn Lee,Rogerio Feris,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DocHop基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在文档式图像中结合叙述文本与多张图表进行多跳推理的能力。实验显示，当前模型与人工表现仍存在显著差距，且推理复杂度上升会明显降低模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表问答和文档问答基准通常彼此独立，难以检验模型能否先依据文本上下文确定语义参照与目标实体，再从一个或多个图表中选择、解释并聚合证据。论文旨在填补这种“文本约束驱动的图表证据推理”评测空白。

Method: 作者构建DocHop数据集：文档叙述提供多步组合约束，图表提供数值证据，问题要求模型依据叙述中定义的语义参照标签解析目标实体，并跨图表完成证据聚合。数据集通过随机化、逻辑优先的生成流程构造，可控制推理深度和视觉密度；共含2,074个样例，覆盖六类任务。随后在多种闭源与开源多模态大语言模型上进行评测，并分析推理增强模型及不同复杂度条件下的表现。

Result: 人工标注者准确率超过90%，最佳模型仅达到62.83%，表明该任务对现有模型具有较高挑战性。具备显式推理增强机制的模型整体表现更好，但随着多步推理复杂度提高，模型性能持续下降。

Conclusion: DocHop为图文联合、多跳文档推理提供了可控且具有挑战性的测试平台。结果表明，现有多模态模型虽已具备一定图表与文档理解能力，但在利用文本上下文定位实体、跨图表筛选证据和执行组合聚合推理方面仍需改进。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved strong performance on structured visual understanding tasks such as chart and document question answering. However, existing benchmarks typically evaluate these domains in isolation, leaving underexplored a key capability: whether models can use textual context to determine how chart evidence should be selected, interpreted, and aggregated. We introduce DocHop, a benchmark for integrated chart--context reasoning in document-style images. In DocHop, the document narrative specifies multi-step compositional constraints, while charts provide the corresponding data values. Questions are grounded on a semantic reference label defined in the narrative, requiring models to resolve target entities from context before aggregating evidence across multiple charts. To enable systematic evaluation, we construct DocHop via a stochastic logic-first generation pipeline with controllable reasoning depth and visual density, covering 2,074 examples across six task categories. Experiments on a wide range of proprietary and open-source MLLMs show a substantial gap to human performance: annotators achieve over 90% accuracy, while the best model reaches only 62.83%. Reasoning-enhanced models consistently show improved results, but performance degrades as reasoning complexity increases. Overall, DocHop provides a controlled testbed for challenging multi-hop document reasoning.

</details>


### [21] [MineTRACE: An Evidence-Grounded Interactive Reasoning System for Mineral Prospectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02060)
*Yiran Zhang,Jinwen Liu,Daniel Su,Yisu Chen,Qiang Sun,Chris Gonzalez,Eun-Jung Holden,Marco Fiorentini,Wei Liu,Yihao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MineTRACE是一个面向八种矿产的可解释、证据驱动型矿产远景预测与交互式探索系统，将地球化学、地球物理和地质数据融合为可追溯的远景评分，并通过自然语言呈现位置查询结果及其证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矿产远景预测系统通常只提供难以解释的评分或热力图，用户难以理解预测依据、核验结果并高效利用公开地学数据。因此，研究旨在构建一个透明、可交互且证据可验证的矿产勘查辅助系统。

Method: 系统采用基于地质知识和已知矿床构建的透明专家树，融合多源地球科学证据，生成铜、金、镍、钨、锡、钴、钽和锰八种矿产的远景评分。Web系统支持地图浏览、位置或区域查询、证据查看及自然语言交互；对新位置的查询由分析管线检索预测分数和支持证据，再由对话助手生成自然语言回答。评估同时包括空间AUC、查询准确性和回答证据 grounding。

Result: 不同测试场景下，远景评分模型的空间AUC最高达到0.917；端到端评估进一步考察了位置查询准确性以及自然语言回答与底层证据的一致性。摘要未报告各矿种的具体指标、基线比较或统计显著性。

Conclusion: MineTRACE将多源地学数据、可解释专家规则、地图可视化和自然语言交互结合起来，提高了矿产远景预测的透明度、可访问性和可验证性，有望支持更高效的矿产勘查决策。

Abstract: Mineral exploration requires integrating heterogeneous geochemical, geophysical, and geological evidence, yet existing prospectivity systems often provide only opaque scores or heatmaps. We present MineTRACE, a web-based system for evidence-grounded exploration of eight commodities: Cu, Au, Ni, W, Sn, Co, Ta, and Mn. Users can explore prospectivity maps, query locations or regions, inspect supporting evidence, and interact through natural language. A transparent expert tree, informed by geological knowledge and known deposits, combines multi-source evidence into interpretable prospectivity scores. For a new location, the conversational assistant retrieves the score and supporting evidence from the analysis pipeline and presents them in natural language. The scorer achieves spatial AUC values of up to 0.917 across different test scenarios, while end-to-end evaluation assesses query accuracy and response grounding. MineTRACE makes public geoscience data easier to access, interpret, and verify, supporting more efficient and transparent mineral exploration.

</details>


### [22] [CHIME: Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution for Long-Horizon Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02074)
*Yongshi Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Muyang Ye,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Longyue Wang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CHIME（Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution），通过先进行结果归因、再写入分层记忆库，缓解自演化记忆方法将任务失败错误归咎于规划的信用分配问题，从而以较低推理与训练成本提升长程任务规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时搜索通常推理开销较大，训练式规划依赖昂贵数据；自演化记忆虽可在不更新参数的情况下积累经验，但往往仅依据最终任务结果更新记忆。由于最终结果同时受规划质量、执行误差和环境因素影响，这种粗粒度反馈会导致规划经验与执行经验混杂，形成有偏且噪声较大的记忆。

Method: CHIME维护彼此独立的规划记忆库与执行记忆库，并采用“先归因、后记忆”（attribute-before-memorize）流程。对于每个任务结果，框架先判定原因主要归属于规划、执行、二者共同作用或均不归属，再仅更新与归因结果对应的记忆库。该分层更新机制旨在使规划模块获得更纯净的规划反馈，同时避免执行或环境扰动污染规划经验。

Result: 在四个长程智能体基准上，CHIME稳定优于训练式方法和已有自演化记忆基线。分析表明：CHIME能以更少的记忆条目积累有效经验；记忆价值与其实际下游效用一致，高质量规划记忆通常比执行记忆更有价值；所积累的记忆还能迁移至不同骨干模型。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将信用分配显式纳入记忆演化过程，并通过规划—执行双记忆库实现针对性更新。结果说明，提升记忆质量比单纯增加记忆规模更关键；可靠的结果归因可显著增强无参数更新条件下的持续规划改进与跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Planning is a central capability that enables agents to decompose complex long-horizon tasks into manageable steps. Test-time search and training-based methods improve planning but incur high inference costs or require expensive training data. Self-evolving memory instead accumulates reusable experience from agent interaction outcomes into an external memory bank, so planning capability keeps improving at inference time without parameter updates. However, existing self-evolving memory methods share an inherent credit assignment problem: they rely on final task outcomes as feedback, but such outcomes conflate plan quality with execution errors and environmental factors, so the accumulated planning experience is often biased and noisy. To address this problem, we propose Credit-Aware Hierarchical Memory Evolution (CHIME), a self-evolving memory framework that maintains a separate planning bank and execution bank and follows an attribute-before-memorize principle: CHIME first attributes each task outcome to the plan, the execution, both, or neither, and then updates only the corresponding memory bank. Extensive experiments on four long-horizon agent benchmarks show that CHIME consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-based and self-evolving memory baselines. Further analyses reveal several interesting findings. For example, CHIME accumulates effective memory with far fewer items. In addition, the learned memory values faithfully reflect downstream utility: high-quality planning memories are more valuable than execution memories. Finally, the accumulated memory effectively transfers across backbone models. Code will be released at https://github.com/ATH-MaaS/Marco-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [23] [Beyond Outcome Gaps: Process-Aware Fairness Diagnosis for LLM-based Multi-Agent Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02092)
*Yiran Zhao,Lu Zhou,Liming Fang,Yufei Chen,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCOPED-Hiring提出一个面向招聘多智能体系统的过程感知公平诊断框架，通过分析超过31.1万条结构化决策轨迹，揭示最终录用率平衡并不代表决策过程公平；针对性修复可将分层负担降低72.3%，且录用率仅变化1.86个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于最终结果的公平审计难以定位多智能体招聘决策中的具体风险来源，可能遗漏简历评估、调查路径和代理线索使用等过程性不公平问题。因此，论文旨在建立能够追踪并量化决策轨迹公平性的诊断方法。

Method: SCOPED-Hiring构造受控的简历变体，运行具有不同角色的招聘委员会，并记录超过31.1万条结构化决策轨迹。研究将轨迹字段转化为量化公平信号，并从最终结果、反事实、过程、路径、动态和设计六个诊断视角进行分析；随后依据诊断结果实施有针对性的系统修复。

Result: 实验发现，即使最终录用率保持平衡，决策轨迹中仍可能存在隐性不公平：职业空档会引发额外怀疑，代理性线索会影响资格判断，身份相关线索会导致调查程度不均。针对性修复使总体分层负担降低72.3%，同时录用率仅改变1.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 论文表明，多智能体招聘系统的公平性不能仅通过最终录用结果评估，还应结合过程和决策路径进行诊断。SCOPED-Hiring能够定位隐藏的轨迹级风险，并以较小的结果变化实现显著的公平改进，为高风险LLM多智能体系统的审计与修复提供了可操作框架。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly considered for high-stakes decision-making, yet outcome-based fairness audits can miss where risks arise within the decision trajectory. We present SCOPED-Hiring, a process-aware fairness diagnosis pipeline for LLM-based hiring MAS. SCOPED-Hiring constructs controlled resume variants, runs role-based hiring committees, logs over 311K structured decision trajectories, and converts trajectory fields into quantitative fairness signals organized by six diagnostic lenses: final outcome, counterfactual, process, pathway, dynamic, and design effects. SCOPED-Hiring reveals that balanced final hire rates can mask hidden trajectory unfairness in multi-agent decision trajectories: career gaps trigger suspicion, proxy cues shape qualification judgments, and identity cues lead to unequal investigation. Targeted repair guided by these diagnoses reduces total layered burden by 72.3% while shifting the hire rate by only 1.86 pp, showing that process diagnosis can guide effective repair. Project Page: https://scoped-hiring-project-page.vercel.app/

</details>


### [24] [MASkills: Continual Skills Optimization for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02094)
*Huaiyuan Yao,Xiaoou Liu,Charles Fleming,Tianlong Chen,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出MASkills，一个面向大语言模型多智能体系统的持续学习框架。其核心是以可执行、结构化的“智能体技能”替代难以调用与扩展的经验记忆，并通过面向技能的归因和优化机制持续演化技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统虽能完成复杂任务，但难以从交互经验中稳定持续改进。基于自我反思的记忆方法通常存在检索调用困难、内容难以细化、规模扩大后管理成本高等问题。作者认为技能比自由文本记忆更具操作性，因为技能可明确描述适用条件、执行步骤及所需资源或工具。

Method: MASkills构建了一个技能驱动的优化流水线：首先根据任务执行过程进行技能条件化归因，将任务表现关联到所使用的技能；随后通过分层归因聚合，处理不同智能体、步骤和技能层级对整体结果的贡献；最后采用动量平滑优化以降低单次反馈噪声并提升更新稳定性。技能库可经历细化、归纳、整合和剪枝，从而逐步形成更有效且更紧凑的程序性知识集合。

Result: 论文在HotpotQA、LoCoMo和GAIA三个任务基准上评估MASkills，结果表明该框架能够在多类智能体任务中取得有效改进。摘要未提供具体数值、对比方法及统计显著性，因此无法仅据摘要量化其相对增益或判断不同模块的独立贡献。

Conclusion: MASkills将多智能体持续学习的主要知识载体从经验记忆转为可执行技能，并结合归因、聚合与平滑更新机制实现技能库的持续演化。该思路有望提升多智能体系统的可复用性、可维护性与长期适应能力；其实际泛化范围、计算开销和技能演化质量仍需结合全文实验与消融研究进一步验证。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on complex tasks, yet continual improvement from interaction experience remains challenging. Existing self-reflection methods build experience memories, but memories are mostly hard to invoke, refine, or scale, while agent skills offer a more actionable unit: structured procedural knowledge that specifies when to act, how to act, and which resources or tools to use. We introduce MASkills, a continual learning framework that optimizes multi-agent LLM systems through agent skills. MASkills presents a new agent-optimization pipeline that integrates skill-conditioned credit assignment, hierarchical credit aggregation, and momentum-smoothed optimization, enabling agent skill libraries to evolve through refinement, induction, consolidation, and pruning. Experiments on HotpotQA, LoCoMo, and GAIA demonstrate the effectiveness of MASkills across multiple agentic tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/DaRL-GenAI/MASkills

</details>


### [25] [READY or Not: Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02095)
*Veronica Chatrath,Bryan Zhu,Jingxuan Fan,George Pu,Soham Dinesh Tiwari,Soham Dan,Ryan Young,Yuan,Li,Yuang Yao,Apaar Shanker,Minglai Yang,Daniel Yue Zhang,Yunzhong He,Ying Liu,Chenguang Wang,Zhijun Yin,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: READY是一个面向企业部署的AI智能体资格评估框架，不仅衡量任务完成率，还评估在特定可靠性目标下所需的人类监督负担与运行成本，并通过统计检验确认部署资格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准主要关注能否完成专业任务，但企业部署还必须考虑可靠性、人类监督程度和成本。仅比较自主准确率可能掩盖不同系统在达到相同可靠性要求时的实际部署代价，因此需要统一且可检验的部署评估方法。

Method: READY输入智能体、工作流和候选监督策略，保留各工作流自身的成功定义，统一测量人机系统的可靠性与运行成本，并选择满足指定可靠性目标的最低成本监督策略。随后在留出案例上进行统计资格认证，生成包含可靠性、监督负担和成本的部署画像。该框架将工作流定义、执行、评估和资格认证解耦，并可运行于现有智能体评测基础设施。

Result: 在涵盖16个智能体系统和750个案例的端到端临床审计案例中，READY发现自主性能相近的系统可能具有显著不同的部署要求：自主准确率为72.8%和72.5%的两个系统，在相同76%可靠性目标和所评估监督策略下，分别需要39.2%和29.6%的人工复核。

Conclusion: READY将企业智能体评估从单纯比较任务表现，扩展为评估其在何种监督条件和成本下能够可靠部署。通过明确并统计检验这些部署条件，该框架可用于比较智能体系统、制定监督要求，并支持基于证据的部署决策。

Abstract: An AI agent can perform well on benchmarks and still be unsuitable for deployment. Existing AI-agent benchmarks measure whether an agent can complete realistic professional work, whereas enterprise deployment asks a different question: whether an agent can meet a required reliability level, under acceptable human oversight, and at tolerable cost. We introduce Reliable Enterprise Agent Deployment (READY), a framework for qualifying AI agents for deployment on enterprise workflows. READY preserves each workflow's own definition of successful execution while applying a common qualification procedure. Given an agent, a workflow, and a class of candidate oversight policies, READY measures the reliability and operating cost of the human-AI system, selects the minimum-cost policy that satisfies a specified reliability target, and statistically qualifies it on held-out cases. The resulting deployment profile characterizes the supported operating point: reliability, human-oversight burden, and cost. READY is implemented as an open testbed that decouples workflow specification, execution, evaluation, and qualification, and runs on existing agent-evaluation infrastructure. In an end-to-end clinical-audit case study spanning 16 agent systems and 750 cases, READY reveals differences hidden by autonomous performance: two systems separated by only 0.3 points in autonomous accuracy (72.8% vs. 72.5%) require 39.2% versus 29.6% human review, respectively, to qualify at the same 76% reliability target under the evaluated oversight policy. READY thus shifts enterprise agent evaluation from how well can the agent perform the work? to under what conditions, and at what cost, can it be reliably deployed? By making those conditions explicit and statistically testable, READY provides a basis for comparing agent systems, setting oversight requirements, and making evidence-based deployment decisions.

</details>


### [26] [Semantic Signal-Assisted Inspection and Recovery Allocation in Reverse Logistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02116)
*Jiani He,Dingyan Shang,Yihua Xu,Shiqi Huang,Yan Lyu,Jize Li,Shangjing Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出语义信号辅助决策支持框架，将退货备注转化为状态因子和信号质量评分，用于在劳动力受限时决定检查深度与回收分配。关键词方法在三个合成场景中降低检查成本并相较结构化特征基线提高净回收价值，但完全跳过检查的风险盲基线在纯经济目标下表现更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向物流决策通常需要在资产真实状况尚未完全明确时进行，而全面检查会消耗有限的人力资源。研究旨在利用退货文本中的叙述性信息，改进检查资源配置和回收决策。

Method: 构建语义信号辅助决策框架，从退货备注中提取资产状况因子与信号质量评分，并据此指导检查优先级、检查深度和回收分配。在信息技术设备退役、航空器维护和消费电子退货三个合成基准场景中，使用30组配对仿真种子，将关键词提取方法与带有噪声全面检查的结构化特征比较器及完全不检查的风险盲比较器进行对比，同时评估短语提取器和大语言模型提取器。

Result: 关键词方法在三个场景中均相较结构化特征比较器提高净回收价值并降低检查成本。在相同检查成本下，评分引导的目标检查在航空器场景中每批次增加约5.39万美元价值，但在另外两个场景中的经济影响较小；短语提取器和大语言模型提取器在航空器场景中带来进一步收益。风险盲比较器在纯经济目标下仍取得更高价值，说明检查信息的收益未必能抵消其成本。

Conclusion: 文本叙述信息能够在回收决策前辅助检查资源分配，尤其适用于信号质量较高且检查价值明显的场景。然而，研究基于合成仿真，结果具有场景依赖性；若仅优化经济收益，跳过检查可能更优，因此实际部署还需纳入风险、质量、合规性和决策稳健性等目标。

Abstract: Reverse-logistics operators often decide how to inspect and route returned assets before their condition is fully observed, while full inspection consumes scarce labor. Semantic Signal-Assisted Decision Support converts return notes into a condition factor and a signal-quality score that guide inspection depth and recovery allocation under shared labor capacity. We evaluate the framework in three synthetic benchmark scenarios spanning information technology decommissioning, aircraft maintenance, and consumer-electronics returns. Across 30 paired simulation seeds, the keyword implementation improves net recovery value relative to a structured-feature comparator with noisy full inspection while reducing inspection cost in all three scenarios. A risk-blind comparator that skips inspection altogether still records higher value under the benchmark's purely economic objective. At matched inspection cost, score-guided targeting adds 53.9 thousand United States dollars per batch in the aircraft scenario but has little economic effect in the other two configurations; phrase and large language model extractors provide further gains in the aircraft scenario. These results show how narrative evidence can support inspection allocation before recovery decisions are made.

</details>


### [27] [Beyond Context Windows: Persistent Discovery Context for Data-Centric Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02129)
*Jalal Mahmud*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出持久化发现上下文，一种保存并复用历史“任务意图—数据对象”映射的轻量级记忆层，用于增强数据对象检索。实验表明，该方法在三个结构化数据环境中持续优于仅依赖元数据的搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心型智能体通常在规划或执行前进行数据发现，但已获得的发现结果往往被丢弃，导致重复检索和知识浪费。研究旨在验证历史发现结果能否作为可复用上下文提升后续检索效果。

Method: 构建持久化发现上下文，存储历史意图与相关数据对象之间的映射，并将这些记忆用于增强未来检索。研究在三个结构化数据环境中进行评估，同时比较元数据检索、记忆增强检索和仅依赖记忆的检索，并测试自动生成记忆及其潜在干扰。

Result: 持久化发现上下文在三个环境中均稳定提升检索质量；自动生成的记忆同样有效。研究还揭示了一种可复现的记忆干扰失败模式。在词汇稀疏领域，仅依赖记忆的检索甚至优于基于元数据的检索。

Conclusion: 数据发现结果本身是一种有价值且可复用的上下文资源。将其持久化并用于后续检索能够增强数据中心型智能体的发现能力，但仍需关注记忆冲突和干扰问题。

Abstract: Data-centric agents repeatedly perform a discovery step before planning or execution: identifying the data objects relevant to a task. Yet successful discovery outcomes are typically discarded rather than reused. We introduce persistent discovery context, a lightweight memory layer that stores prior intent-to-object mappings and reuses them to augment future retrieval. Across three structured data environments, persistent discovery context consistently improves retrieval quality over metadata-only search, remains effective with automatically generated memories, and exposes a reproducible interference failure mode. In lexically sparse domains, memory-only retrieval can even outperform metadata-based retrieval. These findings suggest that discovery outcomes constitute a useful form of reusable context for data-centric agents.

</details>


### [28] [EmoStance: Response-Side Affective-Orientation Control for Empathetic Response Generation via Emoji Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02133)
*Ziyuan Jin,Yuxuan Ge,Zheng Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 EmoStance：利用多标注者给出的表情符号分布作为弱监督信号，学习“回应方情感取向”的潜在控制空间，并通过连续前缀嵌入引导冻结的指令微调语言模型生成更具共情性和针对性的回复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情回复生成通常侧重识别说话者情绪或生成特定情感内容，但较少显式建模倾听者应以何种态度回应。论文将这一问题定义为回应侧情感取向控制，旨在提升回复对上下文情境的贴合度与被感知的回应性。

Method: 作者构建 EmojiDialogue 数据集，在 EmpatheticDialogues 的话语级样本上补充表情符号投票及其置信度。EmoStance 同时建模源说话者的情感表达，并基于对话上下文和说话者角色预测回应侧的软情感取向；随后以连续前缀嵌入将该控制信号注入冻结的指令微调大语言模型。表情符号分布仅作为弱的情感—态度证据，而非生成目标或硬标签。

Result: 在包含20名标注者、800次判断的盲测两两比较中，EmoStance 获得62.2%的决定性胜率。其最明显的提升体现在上下文特异性和主观感知的回应性；同时，该方法与引入外部知识的方案具有互补性。

Conclusion: 通过将群体表情符号分布转化为潜在的回应态度监督，并以软控制方式调节语言模型，EmoStance 为共情回复中的“如何回应”提供了有效建模路径。结果表明，显式控制回应方立场有助于生成更贴合对话语境的回复，但摘要未说明自动评测、消融实验、数据规模及跨场景泛化表现，仍需结合全文进一步验证。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation requires models to decide not only what to say, but also how to respond to the previous speaker's affective situation. We formulate this as response-side affective-orientation control and use multi-annotator emoji distributions as weak affective--attitudinal evidence, rather than as output symbols or gold labels, to induce a latent control space that operationally approximates listener stance. We construct EmojiDialogue, an utterance-level extension of EmpatheticDialogues with emoji votes and confidence scores, and propose EmoStance, which models source-side affective expression, predicts a soft response-side orientation from dialogue context and speaker roles, and steers a frozen instruction-tuned LLM through continuous prefix embeddings. In blind pairwise evaluation with 20 annotators and 800 judgments, EmoStance achieves a 62.2% decisive win rate, with the clearest gains in contextual specificity and perceived responsiveness, while remaining complementary to external-knowledge methods. Code, annotation metadata, and reconstruction scripts are available in our GitHub repository: https://github.com/18277390221/EmoStance.

</details>


### [29] [FUSE: An Evaluating Framework for Dangerous Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02168)
*Zhengyi Jin,Ru Zhang,Xiao Chen,Xinbo Liu,Jiaxuan Lin,Jia Huang,Jianyi Liu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出统一、模块化的危险人工智能能力评估框架，通过知识（K）、防御（D）和危害（H）三条正交管线，生成标准化能力画像，并以化学—生物领域评估12个商业大语言模型，辅以网络安全试点验证框架的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有危险能力评估较为碎片化，难以在不同模型、领域和时间点之间进行一致比较，也难以区分模型的知识水平、防御能力与潜在危害输出。论文旨在建立统一协议，支持跨领域、跨模型和跨时间的系统化评估。

Method: 框架将每个模型分别置于K、D、H三条评估管线中，并聚合为危险能力画像φ。模块可插拔地提供场景种子、知识库、风险查询和评审标准，而核心评估引擎保持不变。研究使用化学—生物模块测试来自四个模型家族的12个商业大语言模型，并通过网络安全试点检验协议迁移性；同时采用多评审者一致性和管线间相关性分析验证可靠性。

Result: 不同模型呈现明显不同的能力画像：知识水平相近的模型在拒答稳健性上存在显著差异，防御能力较强的模型在生成相关内容时并不必然产生更少的危害性输出；Claude、DeepSeek和GPT模型家族也表现出不同的整体模式。按发布时间分析发现，危险能力并未单调下降：较新的模型知识能力增强，但防御能力仅部分改善，模型扩展与对齐进展并不总能转化为一致的安全提升。评审者一致性较高，bootstrap相关系数ρ>0.79（5名评审者中4名达到该水平）；K、D、H之间的相关系数为0.32至0.52，支持三条管线具有一定正交性。

Conclusion: 该框架为危险人工智能能力提供了可复用、可扩展和可比较的评估基础。研究表明，单一安全指标不足以刻画模型风险，必须同时考察知识、防御与危害三个维度；未来模型安全评估应重视能力增长与防御改进之间的不一致，并持续开展跨领域、跨时间的联合监测。

Abstract: Fragmented safety evaluation undermines the governance of dangerous AI capabilities. We present a modular framework that evaluates each model through three orthogonal pipelines---Knowledge ($K$), Defense ($D$), and Harm ($H$)---under a unified protocol, aggregating results into a standardized dangerous-capability profile $φ$. Pluggable modules supply scenario seeds, knowledge banks, hazard queries, and judge rubrics, while the core evaluation engine remains unchanged across domains; the CB evaluation is complemented by a cyber pilot demonstrating protocol transfer.
  Instantiating the framework with a chemical-biological (CB) module, we evaluate 12 commercial LLMs from four families. Our first contribution is a horizontal comparison of dangerous capability across models and model families: the three dimensions expose sharply divergent profiles---models with comparable knowledge differ in refusal resilience, and strong defenders do not generate less harmful content when they do comply---while family-level patterns further separate Claude, DeepSeek, and GPT models. The second is a temporal analysis of capability evolution: tracking $K$, $D$, and $H$ against model release dates reveals that dangerous capability has not monotonically declined; newer models deepen knowledge while only partially improving defense, showing that scaling and alignment progress do not uniformly translate into safety. Reliability is established via cross-judge consistency (bootstrap $ρ> 0.79$, 4 of 5 judges) and pipeline orthogonality ($K$--$D$--$H$ inter-correlations $ρ\in [0.32, 0.52]$).

</details>


### [30] [Examining the Vulnerability of Multi-Agent Medical Systems to Human Interventions for Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02191)
*Benjamin C Liu,Dillon Mehta,Rishi Malhotra,Adam Zobian,Yong Ying Tan,Samir Chopra,Daniella Rand,Natalie Pang,Abhiram Gudimella,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，在多智能体医疗诊断对话的关键“故障点”实施人工干预，可显著改变诊断推理轨迹与准确率；正确干预最高可使基线准确率提升40%，而错误或带偏差的干预会导致性能下降、诊断漂移和不确定性增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体医疗系统的诊断过程会受到对话中外部输入的影响，但何时干预最有效、错误干预会造成何种风险尚不明确。研究旨在定位推理最易受影响的关键时刻，并评估人工引导对诊断稳健性的作用。

Method: 研究将“故障点”定义为智能体对话中推理最容易受到外部影响的时刻，基于MedQA数据集构建模拟医患对话。在这些节点施加正确、错误及偏差相关的人工干预，并比较干预前后的诊断准确率、推理偏移程度与不确定性变化，同时分析智能体行为与临床认知偏差的相似性。

Result: 正确干预最多可将基线诊断准确率提高40%。错误或偏差相关干预最多会使性能降低6%，并增加诊断漂移和不确定性。系统还呈现出与临床实践中认知偏差相似的行为模式，包括过早结论和对误导性线索的易感性。

Conclusion: 识别多智能体医疗对话中的故障点，并在这些节点实施恰当的人类引导，有望提升诊断准确性与鲁棒性。但系统对错误信息和偏差性提示仍较敏感，因此实际应用需要设计可靠的干预规范、风险控制与验证机制。

Abstract: Human interventions at fault points can alter the diagnostic accuracy of multi-agent medical systems. We defined fault points as moments in AI agent conversations, in which an agent's reasoning became most vulnerable to external influence. Using the MedQA dataset, this study analyzed simulated doctor-patient conversations to measure how interventions shifted reasoning and accuracy. Correct intervention methods showed an improvement in baseline diagnostic accuracy of up to 40%, while incorrect or bias-related interventions degraded performance by up to 6% and increased diagnostic drift and uncertainty. Beyond performance changes, our analysis revealed behavioral similarities between cognitive biases in simulated agent environments and real-world clinical practice. Examples included premature closure and susceptibility to misleading cues. Overall, these findings demonstrate that identifying and guiding fault points with human interventions may provide a mechanism for improving diagnostic robustness in multi-agent medical systems.

</details>


### [31] [ASCII Attack: Recontextualising Harmful Requests as Artistic Critique in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02215)
*Da Cheng Gu,Yifei Dong,Xinghao Yang,Yongshun Gong,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究一种将有害请求重新呈现为ASCII艺术的单轮黑盒攻击，发现仅改变表面形式和语境就能显著降低安全拒答率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐主要覆盖直接、字面化的请求，对语境重构和呈现形式变化的泛化能力不足，因此需要评估模型是否能识别隐藏在艺术表达中的相同意图。

Method: 提出ASCII Attack：将完全可读的有害请求排版为ASCII字符艺术，并要求模型进行艺术评论；为每个攻击提示配对直接提问控制组，以隔离主题、模型和解码差异。实验覆盖11个模型、8类有害主题，并使用5种危害评估器进行判断。

Result: 框架提示被危害感知分类器判定为有害的比例为62%，控制组为42%；最易受攻击模型的成功率达到93%。在5个评估器中的4个上，单次查询效果达到或超过已发表的单次攻击。攻击效果主要随模型变化，而非主题变化，并未随模型规模扩大而减弱。近三分之二的框架样本中至少有一个评估器不同意多数评估器，显示评测结果存在明显的测量有效性问题。

Conclusion: 安全对齐对表面形式的依赖使模型容易受到语境重构攻击；ASCII艺术形式无需隐藏文本即可诱导模型输出原本会拒绝的内容。研究同时表明，模型间差异和评估器不一致性是解释攻击成功率时必须考虑的关键因素。

Abstract: Safety alignment trains large language models to refuse harmful requests stated plainly, but that training is applied mostly to surface form. Requests that only recontextualise the same operational content, changing how the model reads it, are therefore only weakly covered. The ASCII Attack is one such recontextualisation. It is single-turn and black-box: one message, with no access to model internals. It embeds a fully legible harmful request in ASCIl-art characters, presents it as artwork, and asks for feedback. Unlike ArtPrompt, it hides nothing: the request stays readable. The reply is written as artistic critique and can contain operational detail that a plain request would have been refused for. Every framed prompt is paired with a direct-question control, so the contrast is isolated from topic, model and decoding variation. The contrast identifies a bundled surface, not one isolated channel. Across eleven models and eight harm topics, a harm-aware classifier judges 62% of framed prompts harmful against 42% of controls. On the most susceptible model the framed prompt succeeds 93% of the time. A single query matches or exceeds published single-query attacks under four of five harm judges. The effect tracks the model more than the topic and does not diminish with scale. At least one judge dissents from the panel majority on nearly two-thirds of framed rows, which is itself a measurement-validity finding. That pattern is consistent with mismatched generalisation.

</details>


### [32] [PEARL: Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning with Contextual Subgraphs for Inductive Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02216)
*Yunchi Yang,Longlong Li,Cunquan Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEARL提出一种路径—实体对齐的归纳式知识图谱补全框架，通过查询相关上下文、语义路径检索及路径—实体交互，提升对未见实体缺失链接的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于子图或路径的方法通常独立编码关系路径，忽略路径预测价值会随查询子图结构变化的问题；同时，扩大上下文范围可能引入噪声，因此需要实现上下文感知的路径建模与噪声抑制。

Method: PEARL首先 объедин合查询实体邻域构建查询特定的上下文子图，并利用大语言模型引导的检索器筛选语义相关路径。随后，在路径、上下文实体和全局子图表示之间构建二部交互图，使路径嵌入能够结合局部与全局结构证据进行自适应调整；同时采用双视图对比学习目标，增强随机上下文扰动下的表示一致性。

Result: 在WN18RR、FB15k-237和NELL-995三个基准数据集上，PEARL均取得参与比较的归纳式知识图谱补全方法中最高的平均Hits@10。消融实验、效率分析和案例研究表明，上下文子图建模、语义路径检索、路径—实体交互及对比正则化均对性能提升有效。

Conclusion: PEARL证明了将关系路径置于查询特定结构上下文中进行联合建模的有效性。路径与实体的交互式表示学习结合对比正则化，能够提升归纳式知识图谱补全的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Inductive knowledge graph completion (IKGC) aims to predict missing links involving entities unseen during training, requiring models to learn transferable relational and structural patterns. Existing subgraph- and path-based approaches often encode relational paths independently of their surrounding query subgraphs, although the predictive relevance of a path may vary across structural contexts. We propose PEARL, a Path-Entity Aligned Relational Learning framework that models paths as context-conditioned reasoning signals. PEARL constructs a query-specific contextual subgraph from the union of the query entities' neighborhoods and uses a large language model (LLM)-guided retriever to distill semantically relevant paths. It then builds a bipartite interaction graph over paths, contextual entities, and a global subgraph representation, allowing path embeddings to adapt to local and global structural evidence. To suppress noise introduced by the enlarged context, PEARL employs a dual-view contrastive objective that promotes representation consistency under stochastic contextual perturbations. Experiments on WN18RR, FB15k-237, and NELL-995 show that PEARL obtains the best average Hits@10 among the compared IKGC methods on all three benchmarks. Ablation studies, efficiency analyses, and case studies further validate the contributions of contextual subgraph modeling, semantic path retrieval, path-entity interaction, and contrastive regularization.

</details>


### [33] [SkillGLoW: Procedural-Family Skill Consolidation for Self-Improving Agents on Long-Horizon Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02217)
*Ao Yan,Xin Zhang,Jiawei Du,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGLoW提出以“程序族”作为技能复用的核心单位，在局部任务技能与去实例化全局先验之间建立全局—局部协同机制。实验表明，该方法在多领域长时程任务中提升性能、增强跨任务迁移，并显著压缩技能库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我改进代理主要采用单一全局文档或按任务存储的技能池。前者容易退化为泛化但缺乏操作性的原则，后者则规模膨胀且技能绑定于生成它的具体任务，难以迁移到新实例。论文因此试图寻找比“文档”或“任务条目”更合适的复用单元。

Method: SkillGLoW将相关任务聚合为程序族。代理首先从具体任务执行中生成局部技能，再将其聚合并压缩为去实例化的全局程序先验；任务相关的具体细节不被永久存储，而是在每个新任务中重新生成。方法还引入提交门控机制，只有当真实执行验证某个先验不会降低当前技能库表现时，才将其纳入库中。

Result: 在数学推理、终端自动化、软件修复和具身控制四个基准、三种模型上，困难设置相较无技能基线平均提升17.2个百分点；加入局部再生成后提升18.0个百分点，12次持续改进实验均获得正收益。技能库按程序族每族保留一个先验，相比按任务技能池紧凑3.6倍。在相同协议下，SkillGLoW在21个实验单元中的15个优于已发表的单文档优化器；在未见过的ALFWorld任务上，成功率由73.9%提升至83.9%。

Conclusion: 结果支持论文的核心观点：真正可迁移的是解决程序，而非具体任务记忆。全局先验负责跨实例复用，局部再生成负责恢复任务细节，提交门控则抑制错误或退化知识进入库中。该设计在多领域长时程任务上同时改善了性能、持续学习稳定性和存储效率。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly self-improve by writing and reusing textual skills, kept either as one global document or as a flat pool of per-task entries, though most of the evidence comes from domains with structurally similar tasks. On long-horizon workloads where each task demands a different solution, the two forms fail in opposite ways: the document collapses into generic discipline, while the pool inflates and its entries stay bound to the instance that wrote them. We argue the missing unit of reuse is the solving procedure shared by a cluster of related tasks, and build SkillGLoW (Global-Local Weave) around it: the local skills a task writes from its own execution are aggregated into procedural families and compressed into de-instantiated global priors, while the instance detail they hold is regenerated per task rather than stored; a commit gate admits a prior only when real execution shows it does not degrade the deployed library. Across four benchmarks (mathematical reasoning, terminal automation, software repair, and embodied control) and three models, the priors gain 17.2 points (hard) over the no-skill baseline on average, with positive gains in all 12 continual-improvement runs, and 18.0 with local regeneration, while the library holds one prior per procedural family, 3.6x more compact than the per-task pool. Under the same protocol GLoW leads a published single-document optimizer on 15 of 21 cells. Unmodified, the library lifts success on unseen ALFWorld tasks from 73.9% to 83.9%, evidence that what transfers is procedure rather than task memory.

</details>


### [34] [PhoenixNest-Video: Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Agent Framework for Automated Video Interview Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02231)
*Fan Yuxuan,Huang Miaojun,Zhang Haimei,Wu Jingshen,Liu Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PhoenixNest-Video是一种面向视频面试的可解释多模态评估智能体，通过结构化视频证据、量规条件检索和强化学习评分，实现逐项、有证据支撑的候选人评分；其在VInterview-2025上达到91.50%的等级准确率，并优于更大型的专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工面试评估在申请者数量激增时成本高且一致性不足，而现有人工智能方法通常只输出缺乏可追溯依据的分数。因此，研究旨在构建能够结合行为证据、遵循评分量规并支持人工复核的自动化评估系统。

Method: 系统构建语义视频图作为结构化工作记忆，并针对评分量规从视觉、音频和文本流中进行条件检索与跨模态验证。随后，采用基于量规的强化学习训练Scorer，通过量规一致性奖励和分数区分度奖励，使模型学习多级评分量规的判别结构，并生成逐项评分及其证据依据。

Result: PhoenixNest-Video在VInterview-2025上取得91.50%的等级准确率，显著优于规模更大的专有模型。与直接提示大型模型相比，该紧凑型量规驱动智能体与专家评审小组的评分一致性更高，同时能够展示支撑每项评分的具体证据。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将结构化证据记忆、跨模态验证与量规导向强化学习结合，可以提升视频面试自动评分的准确性、一致性和可解释性，为高规模候选人评估提供了更适合人工监督的方案。

Abstract: Interview assessment requires per-criterion judgments grounded in behavioral evidence, yet surging applicant volumes have made human-only evaluation costly and inconsistent, while existing AI approaches yield opaque scores without traceable rationale. We introduce PhoenixNest-Video, an evidence-grounded multimodal agent framework for automated video interview assessment.
