It builds a semantic video graph as structured working memory, performs rubric-conditioned retrieval with cross-modal verification across visual, audio, and textual streams, and produces per-criterion scores anchored to the candidate's materials. A Scorer trained via Rubrics-based Reinforcement Learning with dual rewards for rubric alignment and score-level differentiation internalizes the discriminative structure of multi-level rubrics. PhoenixNest-Video attains 91.50\% grade-level accuracy on VInterview-2025, outperforming substantially larger proprietary models. A compact, rubric-grounded agent therefore scores candidates in closer agreement with an expert panel than direct prompting of much larger models, and exposes the evidence behind each score for human review.

</details>


### [35] [PGPO: Potential-Guided Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02236)
*Yuyao Zheng,Haipeng Sun,Junwei Bao,Lemao Liu,Hongfei Jiang,Yang Song,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出潜势引导策略优化（PGPO），利用组内锚点状态的回报统计估计状态潜势，并通过相邻状态潜势差为中间动作提供更细粒度的优势信号，从而改善多轮智能体任务中的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏终局奖励下，现有基于轨迹结果的步骤级优势仍难以区分失败轨迹中的有效动作与错误动作，导致中间行为获得过于粗糙且同质化的负面反馈。

Method: PGPO在每个 rollout 组内，根据锚点状态及其回报统计估计经验状态潜势，再以相邻状态之间的潜势差计算动作优势。该机制能够跨轨迹传播信用，尤其强化失败轨迹内部的步骤级区分能力，同时保持较低的额外训练开销。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 上的实验表明，PGPO 相较近期基于组的强化学习方法取得了较强的整体性能。进一步分析显示，PGPO能够产生更具信息量的失败侧信用信号，且训练额外开销可忽略。

Conclusion: PGPO通过引入基于状态潜势差的跨轨迹信用传播，有效缓解了稀疏终局奖励下的粗粒度信用分配问题，为多轮智能体任务提供了更精细、更有效的策略优化信号。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective paradigm for LLM post-training, but in multi-turn agentic tasks with sparse terminal rewards, it often provides coarse credit for intermediate actions. To obtain more fine-grained credit assignment, recent work such as GiGPO introduces step-level advantages for intermediate actions. However, these step-level signals still rely on the final outcome of each individual trajectory. As a result, actions within failed trajectories can remain poorly differentiated, so effective actions can receive the same unfavorable credit as erroneous ones. In this work, we propose Potential-Guided Policy Optimization (PGPO) for multi-turn agentic tasks. PGPO estimates empirical state potentials from anchor-state-group return statistics within each rollout group. It then derives action advantages from potential differences between adjacent states, enabling cross-trajectory credit propagation. This provides finer-grained step-level credit assignment, especially within failed trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show strong overall performance relative to recent group-based RL methods. Further analysis provides evidence that PGPO yields more informative failure-side credit signals with negligible training overhead.

</details>


### [36] [Propose to Learn, Learn to Propose: Evaluability-Aware Assistance under Bounded Rationality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02242)
*Yifan Zhu,Sammie Katt,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究具备“可评估性意识”的AI辅助提案规划：提案不仅用于执行任务，还用于探测用户的潜在偏好与评估约束。实验表明，在用户评估能力受限时，兼具信息价值的提案可显著优于只追求即时接受的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助方法通常关注提案质量或用户目标推断，并假设用户能够可靠评估任意提案。但受限理性会使用户因提案距离、复杂度或评估成本而难以判断，从而导致高价值提案未被接受。因此，需要将用户可评估性纳入序贯规划过程。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为ProSE，即带隐藏参数的序贯辅助问题。用户响应采用KL正则化的受限理性二元模型，在价值增益与依赖提案距离的可评估性惩罚之间进行权衡。基于该模型，提出深度为2的贝叶斯自适应规划器ProSE-Plan，根据不同可能响应及其引发的后验信念更新对提案进行评分，并与忽略可评估性及短视策略进行比较。

Result: 理论分析显示，容易被接受的提案与最具信息量的探测提案并不总是一致，这解释了单纯追求接受率的规划器为何表现较差。在受控图模拟中，当用户评估成本成为主要瓶颈时，ProSE-Plan优于无可评估性意识和短视基线。探测—承诺消融实验进一步表明，该方法能够选择简单方法忽略的高信息量提案。

Conclusion: 用户可评估性是AI辅助规划中独立且具有实际意义的维度，应与提案生成质量和用户偏好推断协同考虑。通过将提案同时视为任务干预和信息探测工具，可以提升受限评估条件下的辅助效果。

Abstract: AI assistants often collaborate by proposing candidate edits, plans, or designs that users evaluate before adoption. Existing assistance methods focus on proposal quality or user-goal inference, often assuming that the user can reliably evaluate any proposal, which can fail in practice because of bounded rationality. We study evaluability-aware proposal planning, where proposals serve both as task interventions and as probes for learning latent preferences and evaluation constraints, where the resulting belief updates then guide later proposals. We formalise this setting as ProSE, a hidden-parameter sequential assistance problem, and instantiate it with a KL-regularised bounded-rational binary response model in which acceptance trades off value gain against a distance-dependent evaluability penalty. Analysing the planning consequence of this likelihood reveals that likely accepted proposals and informative probes need not coincide, which explains why planners that only pursue acceptance systematically underperform. We operationalise ProSE with \textsc{ProSE-Plan}, a depth-2 Bayes-adaptive planner that scores proposals by possible responses and response-induced posterior beliefs. In controlled graph simulations, \textsc{ProSE-Plan} improves over evaluability-unaware and myopic baselines when evaluation cost is the bottleneck, and a probe-commit ablation confirms that our approach selects informative proposals that simpler methods miss. Our results thus identify user evaluability as a planning-relevant dimension of AI assistance, complementary to generation quality and preference inference.

</details>


### [37] [Task-Level Natural Language Priors as Learning Signals for Low-Resource LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02244)
*Jian Gao,Xiao Zhang,Xun Zhu,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出先验引导调优（PGT）及其具体方法对比先验引导（CPS），将任务级自然语言先验作为低资源大语言模型训练中的辅助学习信号，而非仅作为输入上下文。实验表明，CPS在多个任务上优于标准微调和提示微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源场景中的训练样本往往存在歧义或信息不完整，导致大语言模型容易学习到合理但错误的模式。现有方法通常只将自然语言先验作为上下文输入，未充分利用其在训练阶段的指导作用，因此需要一种能够直接融入优化过程的先验利用机制。

Method: PGT是一种训练范式，在原有监督目标保持不变的基础上，引入自然语言先验相关的辅助损失。其具体实现CPS同时使用正向和负向先验条件损失：正向损失鼓励模型学习与任务一致的行为，负向损失抑制看似合理但具有误导性的替代答案，从而增强模型的任务辨别能力。

Result: 在AmbiMath、Jigsaw以及MNLI/HANS上的实验显示，CPS整体稳定优于标准微调和提示微调。AmbiMath平均精确匹配准确率达到97.6%；Jigsaw平均Macro F1较标准微调提升9.5个百分点，并在仅使用十分之一训练数据时略高于全量数据的普通微调；HANS中，LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen 2.5 7B的非蕴含准确率分别提升8.3和5.2个百分点，同时保持了相近的MNLI域内准确率。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持论文的核心结论：任务级自然语言先验可以作为有效的辅助学习信号，改善低资源大语言模型训练，尤其有助于处理歧义、分布外泛化和容易混淆的替代模式。该方法在提升鲁棒性的同时基本不牺牲域内性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often struggle when low-resource training data are ambiguous or incomplete. Task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance in such settings, but existing approaches usually treat these priors as input context rather than as learning signals during training. We propose Prior-Guided Tuning (PGT), a training perspective that incorporates natural-language priors as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Under this perspective, we introduce Contrastive Prior Steering (CPS), which keeps the original supervised objective intact while adding positive and negative prior-conditioned auxiliary losses to encourage task-consistent learning and discourage plausible but misleading alternatives. Experiments on AmbiMath, Jigsaw, and MNLI/HANS show that CPS consistently improves over plain and prompt fine-tuning. On AmbiMath, CPS achieves 97.6% average exact-match accuracy. On Jigsaw, CPS improves average Macro F1 by 9.5 percentage points over standard fine-tuning, and with 1/10 of the experimental training data slightly exceeds full-data plain fine-tuning. On HANS, CPS improves non-entailment accuracy by 8.3 and 5.2 percentage points for LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen 2.5 7B, respectively, while maintaining comparable in-domain MNLI accuracy. These results support our central claim: task-level natural-language priors can provide useful guidance as auxiliary learning signals for low-resource LLM training. Our code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [LLM-as-a-Judge Is Not an Oracle: Why Self-Improving Agents Need Deterministic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02246)
*Vansh Wahi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在自我改进智能体的提示优化流程中，LLM评委不应作为最终裁决者，而应降级为辅助信号；系统应以不可被评委覆盖的确定性验证层决定变更是否被采纳。作者据此提出PROCTOR框架，并报告其在生产优化循环中发现的评估失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化器通常以LLM评委给出的分数为目标，因而评委偏差、评测脚本缺陷、标签错误及奖励投机都会被优化器放大。文中给出的案例包括：智能体读取环境中的缓存答案而获得满分、错误标签诱导系统删除正确规则，以及静默解析回退使语法损坏的提示被误判为最优。作者认为，仅修改评委提示词或评分量规不足以解决这一结构性问题。

Method: PROCTOR采用教师—学生式闭环：有状态编排器集中掌握工具访问权限；无状态子智能体负责诊断问题和起草变更，但无权直接执行；作为LLM的教师负责评价候选变更。候选变更的最终采纳还必须通过五类确定性护栏：隔离沙箱、能力互斥的角色设计、优先级高于教师意见的验收检查、冻结留出集，以及将满分视为潜在投机证据的金丝雀测试。

Result: 作者声称，在跨合同分析、合规审查和代码质量等生产场景持续运行的过程中，归纳出11种评估信号失效方式，分属评委偏差、评测与指标缺陷、真实标签错误和奖励投机四类。文中最显著的量化例子是：表面100%通过率实际仅对应68%的真实能力。作者还指出，调整评分量规效果有限，而对输出顺序施加结构约束带来了更可靠的改进。摘要未提供对照实验设计、样本规模、各护栏的独立增益或统计显著性，因此效果强度仍需依赖全文验证。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把LLM评估从单点最终裁决改造为受确定性验证约束的组成部分，并通过权限隔离、独立验收与反投机测试降低优化闭环被错误信号劫持的风险。不过，PROCTOR中的教师仍是LLM评委，因而无法消除其固有误判；该方案更准确地说是通过架构性制衡缩小评委失效的影响范围，而非完全解决LLM评估可靠性问题。

Abstract: Self-improving agent pipelines have a problem at their center. An optimizer rewrites prompts to score higher, and the score comes from a judge that is itself an LLM. That judge has the last word on whether the system is getting better, and our position is that it has not earned it. The judge should be demoted from oracle to advisor: its verdict becomes one input among several, and every change is gated instead by a deterministic verification layer the judge cannot override. We reached this position by building the alternative and running it. Over months of running autonomous prompt-optimization loops in production across contract analysis, compliance review, and code quality, we cataloged eleven ways the evaluation signal failed, in four classes: judge bias, harness and metric failures, ground-truth errors, and reward hacking. Agents achieved perfect scores by reading cached answer keys from their environment, a 100% pass rate concealing 68% true capability. A corrupted ground-truth label caused the optimizer to delete correct compliance rules to agree with it. A syntactically broken prompt was promoted as the winner because a silent parser fallback improved the metric. Attempts to fix the judge by rewriting its rubric plateaued; the only reliable gain came from a structural constraint on its output order. In response we describe PROCTOR, a Teacher-Student loop in which a stateful orchestrator holds all tool access, stateless subagents diagnose failures and draft mutations they cannot apply, and a Teacher grades those mutations under five deterministic guardrails: hermetic sandboxes, capability-disjoint roles, acceptance checks that outrank the Teacher, frozen holdouts, and canary cases engineered so that a perfect score is itself evidence of cheating. We report the failures this prevented, and, because the Teacher is itself an LLM judge, the failures it did not.

</details>


### [39] [APEx: Distillation of Agent Procedural Experience for Adaptive Deep Research Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02253)
*Jie Ding,Rui Sun,Xinyuan Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APEx提出一种分层经验利用框架，将实例级轨迹记忆与类别级程序技能结合，并通过执行器、蒸馏器和规划器的闭环协同及三阶段交替GRPO训练，提升深度研究智能体的持续学习与测试时适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验利用方法存在两类不足：直接检索冗长任务轨迹会增加决策负担；程序技能蒸馏通常与下游策略更新脱节，难以实现有效的持续改进。因此，研究旨在构建兼顾经验压缩、技能迁移和策略适应的统一机制。

Method: APEx采用层次化记忆结构，分别存储实例级交互轨迹和类别级程序技能，并由Executor、Distiller和Planner组成闭环架构。三模块通过三阶段交替GRPO进行优化，以奖励为导向执行技能蒸馏，而非依赖固定提示生成。测试阶段，蒸馏技能作为程序先验，引导Planner进行无真实标签的测试时强化学习，并通过技能一致性正则化抑制策略漂移。

Result: 在7个基准测试中，APEx取得当前最佳性能，相比GPT-5.4提升14.7个百分点，相比最强记忆增强基线提升3.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将轨迹记忆、程序技能蒸馏与测试时策略适应置于统一闭环中，能够有效增强深度研究智能体的长程推理、经验迁移和无监督自我改进能力。

Abstract: Deep research agents augment large language models with external tools to answer complex, long-horizon questions through multi-turn reasoning. Learning from prior experience is crucial for continual improvement, yet existing methods either retrieve verbose task-specific traces that burden decision-making, or distill procedural skills that remain decoupled from downstream policy adaptation. We propose APEx, a hierarchical experience utilization framework that organizes interaction history into instance-level trajectory memories and category-level procedural skills, and couples them through a closed-loop architecture of Executor, Distiller, and Planner. The three modules are optimized via a three-stage alternating GRPO training paradigm, enabling reward-guided skill distillation rather than fixed-prompt generation. At test time, distilled skills serve as procedural priors for online Planner adaptation through skill-guided test-time reinforcement learning, allowing ground-truth-free self-improvement with skill-alignment regularization to prevent policy drift. Experiments on 7 benchmarks demonstrate that APEx achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing GPT-5.4 by 14.7 points and the strongest memory-augmented baseline by 3.0 points.

</details>


### [40] [Codebook Agent: Amortized Topology Design for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02264)
*Jinxi Yu,Yubei Li,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Zhi Zhang,Dong Liu,Wenxiao Zhao,Levina Li,Kai-Wei Chang,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Codebook Agent，通过将成功拓扑压缩为16个离散代码，并结合查询条件预测与代理重排序，实现多智能体LLM通信拓扑的快速生成；在六个基准上平均准确率达84.6，同时降低拓扑生成延迟和LLM token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将拓扑设计视为大规模条件图生成问题，存在三个缺陷：奖励筛选后的有效拓扑高度集中；边数与token消耗呈负相关，简单稀疏化可能增加推理成本；在智能体配置相同的常见设置下，基于消息传递的图评分器对邻接结构不敏感，无法有效排序候选拓扑。

Method: 首先使用向量量化自编码器将成功拓扑压缩为查询无关的16项离散代码本。其次，利用奖励加权MLP根据查询嵌入预测代码分布并生成候选拓扑。最后，采用读取展平邻接矩阵的MLP代理模型，联合回归任务效用和归一化token成本，在单次批量前向推理中对候选拓扑进行重排序。测试阶段不使用迭代搜索或消息传递。

Result: 有效拓扑在代码本容量从8增至64时仍约收敛为6种不同图结构。边数与实测token消耗的Pearson相关系数约为-0.4。Codebook Agent在六个基准上平均准确率为84.6，高于最强现有拓扑设计方法的83.0；拓扑生成耗时为2.4毫秒，并减少21.9%至33.2%的LLM token使用量。

Conclusion: 将拓扑设计从连续的大规模图搜索转化为离散代码本预测与轻量代理重排序，能够更符合有效拓扑的实际分布，并同时提升准确率、推理效率和token经济性。该结果表明，结构感知的直接邻接评分器在同质智能体场景下比消息传递评分器更适用。

Abstract: Adapting the communication topology of an LLM multi-agent system to each query improves both accuracy and efficiency, yet current designers treat this as conditional graph generation: a variational, autoregressive, or diffusion decoder searches the $N \times N$ adjacency space, and a graph-network proxy trained on utility and a structural cost such as edge count ranks the sampled candidates. We argue that this formulation is misaligned with the problem. Empirically, topologies that survive a reward filter collapse to about six distinct graphs even when the codebook capacity grows from 8 to 64; edge count is negatively correlated with measured token consumption (Pearson $r \approx -0.4$), so sparsifying the graph makes inference more expensive; and a message-passing scorer over agent-profile nodes is adjacency-invariant whenever agents share a profile---the default configuration of published benchmarks---so it cannot rank candidates at all in that regime. These three facts motivate Codebook Agent: a vector-quantized autoencoder compresses successful topologies into a query-independent 16-entry codebook; a reward-weighted MLP maps the query embedding to a distribution over codes; and an MLP proxy that reads the flattened adjacency, regressed on measured utility and per-task normalized token cost, reranks the top decoded candidates in a single batched forward pass. With no iterative search and no message passing at test time, Codebook Agent is the most accurate method on all six benchmarks we compare (84.6 average against 83.0 for the strongest prior designer), emits a topology in 2.4 ms, and uses 21.9--33.2% fewer LLM tokens.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: CoMerge首先采用如任务算术等朴素合并方法生成质量较差或存在冲突的结果，并将其作为硬负样本；再结合较优行为构造偏好样本对。随后通过偏好优化，仅调整逐张量的合并系数，而非更新全部模型参数。该自监督冲突驱动机制将输出层面的偏好信号映射为参数空间中的冲突缓解。

Abstract: Model merging provides an efficient paradigm for constructing multi-task large language models (LLMs) without full model retraining, yet it remains challenged by parameter interference. While existing methods aim to preserve the capabilities of individual expert models and mitigate interference, they generally do not directly learn from the potentially degraded behaviors exposed by naive merging. In this paper, we propose a conflict-driven preference optimization framework for model merging (CoMerge), which reformulates model merging as a preference optimization problem. The approach utilizes a self-supervised, conflict-driven strategy that leverages the defects of naive merging methods (e.g., task arithmetic) as hard negative samples to construct preference pairs without external annotations. By applying preference optimization to refine lightweight, tensor-wise merging coefficients, CoMerge enables the model to mitigate parameter-space conflicts while preserving task-specific capabilities. Extensive experiments show that CoMerge achieves an average normalized performance of 0.9968 on MergeBench, outperforming all evaluated data-free and data-driven model-merging baselines. *Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov,Mykhailo Shtopko,Dmytro Vodianytskyi,Oleksandr Lukashov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个轻量级GLiClass路由器，在推理时为候选大语言模型打分并选择更适合当前任务的模型，以优化质量、成本和速度之间的权衡。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在六个LiveBench子集上，路由器整体优于候选模型的平均表现。在选取的1000个任务上，其聚合top-1得分为0.707，高于最强固定模型的0.696；不同基准上的提升幅度存在差异。

Conclusion: 结果表明，小参数量、低开销的任务级模型路由器能够有效利用候选模型的能力异质性，在保持推理效率的同时改善总体质量。该方法通过缓存复用、属性预测和可定制标签增强了实际部署适应性，但其收益仍依赖候选模型集合、任务分布及评测基准。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of large language models (LLMs) and the growing diversity of their applications presents a unique optimization opportunity: selecting the right model for the task, while optimizing for speed, cost, and quality at a per-task level. However, inference endpoints can vary widely in quality, price, latency, context support, tool use, domain expertise, and reasoning behavior. This heterogeneity makes manual heuristics difficult to maintain and unlikely to achieve consistently favorable speed--cost--quality trade-offs on their own. We introduce \router{}, a lightweight GLiClass-based router that assigns a suitability score to each inference-time model label without autoregressive generation. The released 0.6B-parameter checkpoint combines a Qwen3 decoder with a shallow bidirectional scorer. Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [43] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出两种提升对齐评估真实性的技术：通过批评反馈优化模拟器行为的“批评细化”，以及用于编码场景、缩小模拟环境与真实部署差距的DISH代理框架。实验表明二者可组合，并能比单纯延长审计更有效地利用额外计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐评估面临评估意识问题：能力较强的模型能够识别自己处于测试环境而非真实部署环境，从而改变行为，削弱安全评估结论的可信度。因此需要构建更难被模型区分的模拟评估环境。

Method: 批评细化为模拟器针对每一步生成多个候选动作，并利用目标模型反馈优化其现实性，随后选择最接近真实部署的候选继续评估。DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）则在编码任务中引入代理式执行框架，使目标模型面对更接近实际部署的交互环境。研究在多个目标模型上分别测试两种方法及其组合效果。

Result: 两种技术均能提升模拟评估的现实性，且具有互补性：联合使用时获得的真实性增益大于单独使用任一技术。实验还显示，将额外计算用于改进模拟行为，比简单延长审计过程更有效。

Conclusion: 自动化方法能够有效缓解评估意识带来的失真问题，增强对齐评估对真实部署行为的刻画能力。批评细化与DISH的组合尤其具有潜力，但其有效性仍依赖目标模型、任务类型和部署环境。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [44] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SafeEvolve提出一种基于经验的智能体安全对齐框架，通过运行时harness与模型策略的持续协同演化，同时改善多步执行安全性与任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常仅更新外部harness或仅进行策略优化，前者难以形成内生安全能力，后者又缺少细粒度的运行时控制，因此无法充分应对智能体最终输出及多步轨迹中的安全风险。

Method: 框架从已完成的在线轨迹中提取安全经验：在harness侧，将轨迹级安全证据转化为受边界约束的组件级更新，覆盖安全提示词与层级技能，并保证更新产物可审计、可回滚；在策略侧，采用两阶段SFT-RL流程，先以harness使用监督微调使策略学会主动利用演化后的harness，再以harness增强的强化学习和验证器分解奖励塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准上，SafeEvolve相较既有基线表现出更优的安全性—效用权衡。以Qwen3.5-4B为例，其在AgentDojo上的攻击成功率降低至原来的约三分之一，同时良性任务效用由59.79%提升至61.86%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将真实执行轨迹中的安全经验同时反馈给运行时控制层与模型策略层，能够形成可持续演进的安全对齐闭环；其关键价值在于兼顾可审计的外部约束、可学习的内在行为改进，以及安全性和任务性能的协同提升。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [45] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SALA，一种语义感知逻辑对齐框架，通过学习任务特定的推理操作并利用动态时间规整对齐推理序列，改进复杂推理任务中的上下文学习示例选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于表面相似度的检索难以捕捉问题背后的解题逻辑；现有逻辑匹配方法依赖预定义推理步骤和严格的精确匹配规则，难以适应灵活、多样的推理过程。

Method: SALA自动诱导任务特定的推理操作，将这些操作嵌入连续语义空间，并采用动态时间规整对不同示例的推理序列进行软匹配和对齐，从而实现灵活且可解释的逻辑检索。

Conclusion: 通过将可学习的推理操作与连续语义表示、动态序列对齐相结合，SALA能够更准确地选择适用于复杂推理的上下文示例，并兼顾匹配灵活性与可解释性。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [46] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AGENTSCOPE是一种面向大语言模型智能体故障诊断的神经符号方法，通过将复杂轨迹抽象为结构化表示，并结合神经不变量与大语言模型推理，定位故障步骤及故障类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体故障通常隐藏在冗长且复杂的执行轨迹中，人工排查效率低下；传统软件缺陷诊断方法难以适配大语言模型智能体，而完全依赖大语言模型进行评判又容易产生不稳定、不可靠的诊断结果。因此，需要一种兼具准确性、可靠性与可解释性的诊断框架。

Method: AGENTSCOPE首先基于智能体执行轨迹构建结构化行为表示，再引入神经不变量描述智能体应满足的行为属性，最后利用大语言模型在结构化表示上进行受约束的推理，将实际行为与神经不变量进行比对，从而识别故障发生步骤及其类型。该方法结合了结构化抽象的符号能力与大语言模型的推理能力。

Result: 在公开的Who&When智能体故障数据集以及作者构建的更全面AgentErrata数据集上，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位和故障归因准确率方面均显著优于现有最先进方法，表明其能够有效处理复杂智能体轨迹中的故障诊断任务。

Conclusion: 将结构化行为抽象、神经不变量与大语言模型引导推理相结合，可以提升智能体故障诊断的准确性、可靠性和可解释性。AGENTSCOPE为复杂大语言模型智能体的故障定位与类型归因提供了一种有效的神经符号解决方案。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [47] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为定量双极论证框架（QBAFs）提出对比式解释方法，用于解释两个主题论证之间的推理结果差异。研究定义了一般性的对比归因函数，并提出基于移除、梯度和Shapley值的具体方法，最后展示其在医疗和偏差识别中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAF解释方法主要针对单个主题论证的推理结果，难以说明两个论证为何产生不同结果。对比式解释能够直接揭示结果差异的来源，并增强论证推理及相关AI模型的可解释性。

Method: 首先定义一般形式的对比归因函数（CAFs），并建立其应满足的一组通用性质；随后分别构造基于特征移除、梯度和Shapley值的CAFs，并分析这些方法的理论性质。

Result: 研究系统化描述了多类对比归因方法，并考察了它们满足通用性质的情况。案例分析表明，这些方法能够用于解释医疗场景中的论证差异，以及识别推理过程中的潜在偏差。

Conclusion: 对比式解释扩展了QBAF的解释能力，使分析重点从单一论证结果转向两个论证之间的差异。移除、梯度和Shapley值方法为不同需求下的差异归因提供了可行工具，并具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [48] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出UTP-Bench，一个面向不确定性旅行规划的大规模基准，利用真实延误与人流数据评估LLM生成行程在随机扰动下的鲁棒性。实验表明，当前主流LLM与人工方案相比，在时间缓冲、交通延误应对和人流感知规划方面仍存在明显差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TravelPlanner和TripCraft等基准主要假设环境确定，仅检查静态约束是否满足，无法评估交通延误、人流波动等现实不确定性下行程是否仍然可执行。因此，研究旨在建立能够衡量旅行计划抗扰动能力的评测框架。

Method: 构建UTP-Bench数据集，覆盖印度504座城市，整合景点、餐饮、住宿及多模态交通网络数据，并引入经验性交通延误分布和人流密度模式模拟随机扰动。提出三个指标：缓冲充足度得分（BAS）、人流感知时间得分（CATS）和交通延误吸收得分（TDAS），用于量化行程对时间、人流和交通变化的适应能力。

Result: 在GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等模型上的实验显示，模型生成方案与人工方案之间存在显著差距。主要问题包括时间缓冲不足、交通安排缺乏延误意识，以及未能根据人流变化合理调整活动时间。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench将旅行规划评估从确定性约束满足拓展至随机环境下的鲁棒性评估，揭示了当前LLM在不确定性感知规划方面的不足。未来模型需要更好地整合延误预测、动态缓冲和人流信息，以生成在现实环境中更可靠的旅行行程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [49] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CivBench是一个基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的开源基准，用于评估语言模型智能体在长时程、工具驱动、部分可观测环境中的规划、监控与执行能力。初步实验表明，智能体普遍存在战略状态监控不足和计划承诺执行不充分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测通常难以覆盖数百轮交互、海量工具调用、长期依赖和部分可观测状态。CivBench旨在提供一个可复现的长时程环境，专门测量智能体在持续规划、状态跟踪和行动执行方面的行为表现，而非简单进行模型排名。

Method: 构建包含300余轮交互、76个MCP工具、大规模动作空间及结构化文本叙述层的环境。研究在四个模型家族上开展23次合规运行，并提出两个接口层指标：主动监控率（PMR），衡量智能体是否主动查询潜在战略状态；RAG@10，衡量计划反思中声明的承诺是否在随后10轮内执行。所有实验采用统一的行动手册协议。

Result: 由于样本规模较小，汇总结果不足以可靠区分不同模型。智能体对可查询但不会自动呈现的战略状态监控不足：尽管行动手册要求每20轮查询一次胜利进度，实际查询间隔为30至75轮；在20次可检测失败中，有7次未能在结束前20轮的预警窗口内完成查询。计划承诺执行率RAG@10在不同模型间为48.2%至65.8%。

Conclusion: 即使具备工具访问权限并获得明确指导，智能体仍会偏离既定指令，表现为战略监控不足和近期计划执行不完整。研究将这些现象解释为指令遵循与执行层面的偏差，而非能力完全缺失。CivBench及其场景、日志、指标和分析流程已公开发布。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [50] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张应将生成式AI语境下的创造力视为人类与算法互动形成的“混合集体”属性，而非个体属性。其核心区分是：新颖性属于单个作品， 多样性属于作品群体；AI辅助通常提高个体新颖性，却可能降低整体多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对于生成式AI究竟促进还是损害创造力存在分歧。作者认为，该分歧主要源于将个体层面的新颖性与群体层面的多样性混为一谈，因此需要建立一个同时考察人机协作、群体组成与传播机制的分析框架。

Method: 摘要提出概念性与机制性论证：区分新颖性和多样性两个创造力维度；将分析单位从个人转向由人类和算法构成、彼此连接的混合集体；比较纯人类、纯模型及人机混合群体的搜索能力，并考察群体中代理者比例、连接结构及机器发现成果进入人类文化后的持续性。

Result: 作者指出，AI辅助构思能够稳定提升单个产出的新颖性，但可能使总体输出趋同、压缩多样性。不过，这并非人机协作的必然结果：由于人类与模型具有互补性的搜索方式，适当构成的混合群体可在新颖性和多样性上同时优于单一类型群体；模型发现的方案也可能被人类社群吸收并延续。

Conclusion: 生成式AI对创造力的影响不应被概括为“有益”或“有害”。决定性因素是混合集体的组成与网络结构，包括参与者类型、比例及其连接方式。未来应设计能将个体新颖性收益转化为群体创新，同时避免集体多样性流失的人机协作配置。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [51] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom 是一种面向根因分析的生成式共识框架，将多个模块化启发式生成的开放式文本假设映射到连续嵌入空间，通过迭代式质心重加权解决冲突，再由轻量级大语言模型完成最终综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中的根因分析需要整合噪声大且相互冲突的文本假设。单体大语言模型存在上下文长度限制、幻觉累积和推理延迟高等问题；传统弱监督方法虽具统计严谨性，却主要适用于离散类别，难以处理开放形式假设。

Method: 系统首先使用可动态填充事件实体、时间和指标的诊断模板生成模块化假设；随后将文本假设投影至连续嵌入空间，利用迭代式质心重加权算法聚合并调节相互冲突的信号；最后依据共识权重调用一次轻量级大语言模型生成根因分析结果。

Result: 在 OpenRCA 基准上，Loom 位于准确率—效率 Pareto 前沿：在 Bank 和 Market-2 数据集上达到与先进自主智能体相当的准确率，在 Market-1 和 Telecom 上略逊一筹。所有数据集每个事件仅需一次大语言模型调用，推理速度约提升 26 倍；使用 8B 参数综合模型时，速度提升约 33 倍。冗余检测未取得理想效果，但确定性共识有助于提升领域专家对结果的信任。

Conclusion: Loom 通过将多智能体式假设生成与连续空间共识聚合相结合，在保持开放式表达能力的同时显著降低推理成本和延迟。研究表明，在工业根因分析中，适度的模块化与确定性共识能够在准确率、效率和可解释信任之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [52] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，“门面效应”在语言模型中并非普遍有效，而取决于模型家族与请求内容。Anthropic前沿模型在先拒绝大请求后更可能接受小请求；OpenAI、Google前沿模型及Haiku 4.5则表现出反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验源于人类社会互动的“门面效应”能否迁移到语言模型，并分析模型在先拒绝较大请求后对相关较小请求的响应机制。

Method: 研究者在三家提供商的九个生产模型上进行实验：先提出较大请求，再提出同一主题的较小请求，并与直接提出较小请求的结果比较；同时设置无关主题拒绝的控制条件，并测试公开基准中的拒绝样本及将请求从“提供可用指令”改写为“解释相关主题”的效果。

Result: Anthropic的Opus 5在经历大请求拒绝后接受小请求的比例为65.8%，直接请求时为29.3%，表现出明显门面效应。OpenAI和Google的前沿模型以及Haiku 4.5则出现反效果，合规率下降15.5至23.0个百分点。无关主题的拒绝在九个模型上均弱于相关主题拒绝，说明让步本身具有作用，但模型对先前拒绝行为的反应存在家族差异。该效应无法迁移到公开基准中的拒绝样本；将265个被拒绝的“提供可用指令”请求改写为同主题的“提供解释”请求后，有263个不再触发拒绝。

Conclusion: 人类影响策略可以迁移到语言模型，但不能视为跨模型通用规律。其效果由模型家族、请求内容及请求表述共同决定；对于降低拒绝率而言，改变任务目标和措辞可能比单纯采用门面效应更可靠。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [53] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出DisCo，一种通过创建、验证并复用“技能”来增强自主机器学习研究代理的方法。基于固定模型、研究框架和执行预算，技能增强代理在多个基准上显著优于无技能代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端机器学习研究代理具备规划、执行、记忆和验证能力，但缺少将方法真正落地所需的领域操作知识。这些知识分散在论文和代码仓库中，面向人类编写且规模过大，难以在任务执行时直接加载和复用。

Method: DisCo采用技能驱动的研究代理架构，支持技能的自动提炼与使用。技能提炼包括两种形式：一是面向任务无关的通用提炼，从广泛使用的机器学习代码仓库中提炼可复用技能；二是面向具体任务的定向提炼，根据任务需求生成所需技能。研究构建了AREX-Skill Library，包含来自1000个常用机器学习仓库的5000多项经验证技能，覆盖20个领域和178个能力族。

Result: 在GPT-5.5骨干模型、研究框架和下游执行预算均保持不变的条件下，加入技能后，代理在MLE-bench上的得分提升134.3%，在PaperBench上提升34.4%，在FrontierCS上提升9.2%，在PassNet上提升14.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将分散的操作知识压缩为经过验证的技能，能够有效增强自主机器学习研究代理，并减少其在每次任务中重新发现实践知识的需要。性能提升主要归因于在固定系统配置下引入了可复用的操作上下文。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [54] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文为多智能体大语言模型的编排—工作者协作与反思记忆更新建立统一理论框架，并提出依赖环境真实反馈的SRMA方法。核心结论是：任务分解质量决定协作均衡误差；仅凭生成文本无法保证反思普遍改进；以环境评估为准的记忆接受机制可获得可证明的收敛性。实验中，完整Kimi系统在500个SWE-bench样例上达到72.2%的解决率，高于70.8%的公开mini-SWE-agent参考结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM系统通常依赖编排器分解任务、多个工作者执行及文本反思迭代，虽有经验效果，但缺少对三项关键问题的统一解释：协作如何形成稳定结果、记忆为何能或不能持续改进、外部验证在反思中是否必要。论文旨在将这些机制形式化，并给出可检验的性能与收敛保证。

Method: 作者将编排器—工作者交互建模为双层协调博弈：在有界耦合条件下，工作者局部更新构成近似势博弈，其均衡松弛量受任务分解质量控制。随后将反思建模为语义记忆状态上的随机迁移，对自由形式反思给出有限时间上界、最坏情形紧性证明，以及在可证伪持续伤害条件下的正下界。针对验证机制，论文证明只观测生成文本的门控器无法在文本不可区分环境中实现一致改进，而基于环境反馈的门控器可以。基于此提出SRMA：仅当有根据的环境评估风险严格下降时才接受候选记忆；并补充随机评估下的置信门控与分段平稳环境下的重新锚定机制。

Result: 理论上，SRMA在校准条件与非退化纠错概率质量成立时可精确收敛，且收敛速度表现为几何或多项式两类；相应构造表明两种速率在阶意义上均为紧的。论文还预测并实验检验协作质量与任务分解、记忆漂移与验证机制之间的关系。实证上，在500个SWE-bench实例中，完整Kimi系统解决率为72.2%，比报告的公开mini-SWE-agent参考系统70.8%高1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献是将多智能体LLM协作、反思记忆和外部验证纳入同一套博弈论与随机过程分析框架。其最重要的实践启示是：反思不能仅依据文本表面质量进行接受，应引入与真实任务结果相联系的环境评估信号；同时，高质量任务分解不仅提升即时执行效果，也会改善多工作者协调均衡。摘要中的实验增益有限但与理论方向一致，具体泛化性仍需结合完整论文中的环境定义、评估成本、基线配置及统计显著性进一步判断。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [55] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向工厂现场设备的本地化检索增强助手，通过结构化压缩、检索增强蒸馏与子网络选择，在有限内存和功耗下实现跨多种边缘设备部署。结果表明，参数规模不能可靠预测适配后的问答质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工厂人员需要便捷查询机器文档，但具备较强能力的模型通常无法适配现场硬件。仅按模型大小、速度或质量进行部署选择，会导致能力或吞吐率明显损失，因此需要一种结合适配后质量、设备性能、通用能力下限和内存约束的选择方法。

Method: 先对模型进行结构化压缩，并采用检索增强适配与蒸馏恢复压缩造成的问答质量损失；随后将部署建模为后适配选择问题，为每类设备选择一个子网络。选择过程同时考虑人工评估的检索增强回答质量、设备实测吞吐率、可配置的通用能力下限和内存预算。为降低候选子网络提取成本，训练采用权重共享的超网络与sandwich-style原位蒸馏。

Result: 在制造业设备手册案例中，模型提取后的评估质量为未剪枝模型的13.7%，检索增强蒸馏后恢复至相差4.6%的水平，挽回约三分之二的质量损失。仅优化模型大小、速度或质量的规则均无法同时保持能力和吞吐率；同一助手可部署于三种异构边缘设备层级，待机功耗为1.3至5瓦。

Conclusion: 结构化压缩结合检索增强蒸馏能够显著缓解边缘部署中的质量损失，而基于适配后实测质量与设备约束的子网络选择，比依赖参数量的传统策略更适合制造现场部署。模型规模与最终检索增强问答质量之间并不存在稳定的单调关系。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [56] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述电信网络根因分析的发展，并提出一种面向5G/6G网络的结构化大语言模型推理框架，通过规范化网络遥测、约束诊断决策路径和生成证据支撑的解释，提升根因识别的准确性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G及未来6G网络具有复杂的跨层依赖关系，传统规则或机器学习方法难以充分处理异构证据。直接使用通用大语言模型又容易产生幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐不足的问题，因此需要面向电信领域设计可靠、可验证的诊断推理机制。

Method: 首先回顾电信网络根因分析从规则方法、机器学习到大语言模型方法的演进，并总结结构化推理、检索增强知识支撑、智能体协同和可验证推理等范式。在此基础上，构建结构化推理框架：将异构网络遥测组织为规范化上下文，在诊断过程中约束决策路径，并生成基于证据的根因解释。

Result: 在TeleLogs和TelecomTS两个5G根因分析数据集上的实验表明，该框架相较基线方法能够持续提升诊断准确率和决策一致性，并在跨数据集测试中表现出稳定收益。

Conclusion: 结构化推理设计是构建实用型大语言模型电信根因分析系统的关键。通过将领域知识、网络证据与受约束的诊断过程结合，可以缓解通用大语言模型在电信故障分析中的幻觉和不稳定问题。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [57] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME（AI情境测量）框架，用于评估AI生成的个体层测量能否恢复情境模型中的个体内与群体间效应。基于2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）的职业分组数据，研究发现，在拥有丰富的个体与职业信息时，AI测量能够较好地复现调查变量中的情境模型信息，但其效果受信息稀缺和多个相关概念同时不可观测等条件限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查往往缺少社会、组织和职业特征变量，而AI可以利用现有丰富数据构造这些测量。但仅验证AI能否预测个体回答，无法判断其是否能够支持情境模型中的群体间比较和群体内偏离分析。因此，研究需要一个同时评估个体层、群体层及情境效应恢复能力的系统框架。

Method: 提出AICOME框架：先在个体层面构造AI测量，再将其分解为群体均值（群体间成分）和个体相对群体均值的偏差（群体内成分），从而估计情境模型中的组间与组内关联。研究使用2022年CFPS，以职业作为经验分组结构，选择计算机使用、外语使用、每周工作时长和管理职责等职业相关变量，将调查测量与AI测量进行回答层面、模型层面、情境层面和边界条件验证。

Result: 总体上，AI情境测量在拥有丰富个体特征和职业特征时，可以恢复调查变量所包含的大部分情境模型信息。每周工作时长的验证效果最强，AI测量成功复现了其与满意度之间显著的职业间负向关联和职业内负向关联。限制输入信息为职业和基本人口特征时，测量性能明显下降；当多个相互关联的概念同时被视为不可观测时，效应恢复能力也会减弱。

Conclusion: AICOME将AI测量从单纯的个体回答预测扩展为对情境模型信息恢复能力的评估，为研究者利用既有丰富数据重建缺失构念提供了方法框架。该方法最适合恢复数量有限且具有明确理论重要性的构念，但其有效性依赖于输入数据的丰富程度以及待测概念之间的关联结构。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [58] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“预测状态匹配”（predicted-state matching）训练目标，使网页世界模型生成的状态表示能够区分不同候选动作导致的结果，从而提升测试时动作选择、动作排序与端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页世界模型通常通过监督式下一状态预测学习HTML或AXTree等固定表示，但该目标未必与下游排序器一致：排序器需要预测状态在不同候选动作之间具有足够区分度。若状态预测虽接近真实表示却缺乏候选间判别性，PRM或动作排序器仍难以正确选择动作。

Method: 作者提出预测状态匹配目标：要求模型预测的状态表示能够将真实动作产生的结果状态与其他替代动作产生的状态区分开。模型使用由WebArena Go-Browse轨迹构建的分支式网页智能体数据集训练，其中每个决策点包含多个候选动作及其对应结果状态。实验将该方法与监督式下一状态预测世界模型进行比较，并应用于预测状态匹配基准、WebPRMBench和WebArena-Lite。

Result: 在留出的预测状态匹配基准上，所提方法优于监督式下一状态预测模型。在WebPRMBench上，将其用于PRM式动作排序时，性能超过仅使用动作信息的PRM，以及结合监督式下一状态世界模型的PRM。在WebArena-Lite测试时动作选择中，该世界模型进一步提升了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 网页世界模型的训练目标应直接优化下游动作选择所需的候选动作判别能力，而不仅是重建下一网页状态。预测状态匹配通过利用分支轨迹中的替代结果状态增强了状态表示的区分性，并在状态评估、动作排序和实际任务执行中表现出一致收益。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>

### [59] [Polynomial Invariants for Probabilistic Transition Systems with Unbounded Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02446)
*Anne Schreuder,Lorenz Winkler,Laura Kovács,C. -H. Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究概率转移系统中多项式不变量的自动综合与验证，基于鞅理论和可选停止定理，提出称为dui的可验证充分条件，以支持具有无界取值范围的随机采样分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率程序不变量方法通常需要较强的有界性或分布假设，难以处理随机样本无界但矩有限的情形。本文旨在建立可处理此类系统的统一验证条件，并实现多项式不变量的自动综合。

Method: 将候选多项式表达式构造成鞅，并应用可选停止定理验证其终止时的期望值等于初始值。具体而言，要求鞅表达式存在可积的支配函数，该条件蕴含一致可积性，作者将其称为dui。在线性概率转移系统中，进一步将dui归约为验证一个与更新矩阵、鞅表达式次数及停止时间相关的期望有限性；若随机样本具有有限矩，且循环运行时间满足相应矩界，则可自动综合满足OST条件的多项式循环不变量。

Result: 提出了一个可操作的dui充分条件及其在线性概率转移系统中的简化判定方法。该方法能够在随机样本具有无界支持但有限矩时，自动综合并验证多项式不变量，从而扩大了可处理的概率程序范围。

Conclusion: 本文将鞅、不变式综合与可选停止定理结合起来，提供了面向线性概率循环的可扩展验证框架。核心贡献是利用一致可积性条件处理无界随机分布；方法的适用性主要依赖于样本矩有限性和运行时间矩界的可验证性。

Abstract: We study the synthesis of polynomial invariants for probabilistic transition systems (PTS) based on martingale theory. We present tractable methods to verify that such polynomials are indeed invariants, in the sense that their expected value upon termination is the same as their value at the start of the computation. We do this by applying the Optional Stopping Theorem (OST) in the form of a specific precondition. This precondition requires the existence of an integrable dominating function for the martingale expression, which implies uniform integrability; we refer to this condition as dui. For linear PTS we simplify the dui property to proving finiteness of the expected value of an expression depending on the update matrix, the degree of the martingale expression, and the stopping time. Specifically, if all random samples have finite moments and we can verify a moment bound on the runtime of a linear loop, then we can automatically synthesise polynomial loop invariants that satisfy the OST. Notably, dui allows for the sampled distributions to have unbounded support, which is a novel contribution to the field.

</details>


### [60] [Specification-Guided Path Shortcutting for Efficient Probabilistic Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02457)
*Tsubasa Matsumoto,Kazuki Watanabe,Masaki Waga*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向概率系统的规范引导路径快捷化方法，通过将满足特定性质的多步转移替换为单步转移，降低马尔可夫链的状态空间规模，从而提升概率模型检测效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式系统通常具有随机性，概率模型检测适用于其安全性验证，但状态空间爆炸，尤其在验证复杂性质时严重影响可扩展性。因此，需要利用待验证性质指导模型压缩。

Method: 针对马尔可夫链和ω-正则性质，识别与固定验证性质相关的可替代转移序列，并将其压缩为单一转移，同时保持性质满足概率不变。研究实现了该路径快捷化方法，并以Storm作为基线进行性能评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在许多基准实例上优于Storm，尤其是在规范较复杂的情况下，能够更有效地提升概率模型检测性能。摘要未提供具体数据、加速比或适用性边界。

Conclusion: 规范引导的路径快捷化可以在不改变性质满足概率的前提下减少模型规模，是缓解概率模型检测状态空间爆炸的有效方法；其优势在复杂规范场景下更为明显。

Abstract: Given the safety-critical nature of many embedded systems, their safety assurance is essential. Because such systems are typically stochastic, probabilistic model checking is a particularly important technique. However, there is a well-known scalability issue due to state-space explosion, especially when verifying complex properties. To mitigate this issue, we propose specification-guided path shortcutting for probabilistic systems, focusing on Markov chains (MCs) and $ω$-regular properties. The key idea is that, when the verified property is fixed, certain sequences of transitions in an MC can be replaced with a single transition without changing the satisfaction probability, and thus, we can reduce the state space of the MC. We implement the proposed path shortcutting and evaluate its contribution to the performance of probabilistic model checking, using Storm as the baseline model checker. The results suggest that our approach often outperforms the baseline, particularly on benchmark instances with complex specifications.

</details>


### [61] [Intuitionistic Unitary Linear Logic: A Proof-Theoretical Approach to Purely Quantum Higher-Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02661)
*Julien Lamiroy,Benoît Valiron,Renaud Vilmart*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出直觉主义酉线性逻辑（IULL），为非因果高阶量子过程提供一种基于Curry–Howard对应的组合式、模块化计算形式化框架，并证明其关键元理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子电路模型无法表示量子开关等非因果高阶过程；现有基于矩阵和线性代数的方法虽能描述其物理性，但主要提供静态、整体性的表征，缺乏组合性与模块化的计算解释。

Method: 以线性逻辑为基础，引入IULL，通过逻辑项和类型系统刻画高阶量子过程，并强调酉性的保持。文章从Curry–Howard对应出发，将证明结构解释为可组合的量子计算过程；同时研究其一致性、完备性及切规则。

Result: 作者证明了IULL的一致性，证明其关于酉变换的完备性，并证明切规则具有可容许性。此外，文章使用IULL重新讨论了若干已知的非因果量子过程，展示了该形式化方法的适用性。

Conclusion: IULL为非因果高阶量子计算提供了区别于静态矩阵模型的逻辑与计算视角，兼具组合性和模块化特征。该工作为描述、推理和构造非因果量子过程建立了形式基础，但其实际表达能力和与更广泛物理过程的对应关系仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Although the circuit model for quantum computation is well established, it is incapable of representing non-causal higher-order quantum processes such as the quantum switch. If several models of non-causal quantum computation have been considered in the literature, the approaches have so far only been focusing on the physicality of such processes, using matrices and other techniques from linear algebra. If these approaches are expressive, they however only provide a static and monolithic understanding of these processes.
  In this article, we propose a new formalism for non-causal, higher-order quantum processes. Based on a Curry-Howard interpetation, our proposal offers a computational interpretation that is both compositional and modular. In particular, we present Intuitionistic Unitary Linear Logic (IULL), a logic based on linear logic focusing on conservation of unitarity for higher order terms. We prove the coherence of IULLL, its completeness with regard to unitaries, and the admissibility of its cut rules. We finally discuss the validity of our approach by revisiting known non-causal quantum processes with IULL.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [Context Inference Attacks Without Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01663)
*Prince Jha,Samuele Poppi,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体在推理阶段通过工具调用组装隐藏上下文时的隐私泄漏问题，提出上下文推断攻击，并证明该攻击在已知、未知及代理自主检索上下文的场景下均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私攻击研究主要关注通过越狱诱导模型直接泄露敏感内容，较少考虑智能体自行调用工具并构建隐藏上下文的情况。即使系统设置了禁止披露指令、logit抑制或上下文稀释等防护措施，模型输出中仍可能保留可被利用的上下文信号。

Method: 作者将上下文推断攻击形式化为安全博弈，并评估三种场景：已知上下文、上下文内容未知，以及由智能体通过工具调用自行检索上下文。实验涵盖灰盒设置（使用目标模型对观测结果评分）和黑盒设置（使用攻击者控制的替代模型评分），同时分析查询预算、上下文规模和目标模型规模对泄漏的影响。

Result: 同一种攻击无需修改即可适用于三种场景。在小候选集上，已知上下文的攻击成功率达到100%；候选集为1024时达到63%。在模板和周围记录未知时，AUROC为78.9%；使用14B替代模型评估32B目标模型时为92.5%；当记录由代理检索返回时为81.8%。这些结果显著高于随机基线：候选识别任务为1/|Z|，二分类任务为50%。

Conclusion: 智能体即使不直接输出敏感上下文，其行为和回答仍可能泄露足以支持上下文识别的信号。现有的指令约束、logit抑制和上下文稀释防护不足以抵御此类攻击，智能体系统需要专门面向工具调用、隐藏上下文和黑盒推断的隐私保护机制。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly deployed to process sensitive data at inference time, such as healthcare records or financial documents assembled into a hidden \emph{context} before the system answers. Prior work has studied privacy risks primarily through \emph{jailbreaking} attacks that induce models to directly disclose sensitive content, but has largely overlooked the agentic setting where the context is assembled by the agent's own tool calls. We show that the agents we evaluate remain vulnerable to hidden-context leakage despite the controls we test against them, namely an instruction not to disclose the context, logit suppression, and context dilution. For instance, a web-browsing agent answering benign user queries still carries exploitable signals about records silently loaded into its context. We introduce and formalize \emph{context-inference attacks} through a security game and evaluate three settings under decreasing attacker knowledge and increasingly indirect delivery of the context: a known context, an unknown context, and a context the agent retrieves through its own tool calls. We distinguish a grey-box setting, in which the target model is used to score observations, from black-box settings in which the attacker scores with a surrogate it controls. We further characterize how leakage varies with query budget, context size, and target-model size. A single attack carries through all three settings without modification, reaching $100\%$ ASR on small candidate sets and $63\%$ at $1024$ candidates against a known context, $78.9$ AUROC when the template and surrounding records are unknown, $92.5$ AUROC when a 14B surrogate scores a 32B target, and $81.8$ AUROC when the records arrive as an agent's retrieval returns, against chance rates of $1/|\mathcal{Z}|$ and $50$ respectively.

</details>


### [63] [Private Computation Space: Experience with Trusted Multi-Cluster Federated Learning for Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01667)
*Shuangyu Lei,Muhammad Salman Abid,Jacob Belding,Sam Mosher,Manushi B. Trivedi,Shivranjani Baruah,Liam Wickes-Do,Andrew Anderson,Braulio Dumba,Alyssa Whitcraft,Ritvik Sahajpal,Sijin Li,Kelly Robbins,Michael Gore,Margaret Frank,Steven Wolf,Liz Jones,Abraham Stroock,Kaitlin Gold,Hakim Weatherspoon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出面向农业场景的开放源代码隐私保护机器学习系统Private Computation Space（PCS），结合异步联邦学习、差分隐私和可信执行环境，在脆弱农村网络与普通硬件条件下实现安全协作建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业人工智能的应用受到农户数据隐私担忧的显著限制。现有联邦学习方案难以同时满足农业环境中的数据与身份保护、模型效用、低成本硬件部署、系统可靠性及农村基础设施适应性要求。

Method: 设计并部署PCS系统，采用多集群编排提升农村环境下的可靠性，并结合异步联邦学习、差分隐私和可信执行环境保护数据及参与者身份。系统在两个真实农业工作负载上进行评估：纽约州基于活体植物传感器的氮监测，持续六个月；加利福尼亚州基于气象站的蒸散量预测，持续十个月。

Result: 氮监测任务取得0.71的Dice相似系数，蒸散量预测取得0.84的R²。相较于最差的单站点模型，协作模型准确率分别提升22.4%和9.1%，同时保持隐私保护。

Conclusion: PCS表明，将异步联邦学习、差分隐私和可信执行环境结合，并针对农村基础设施进行系统设计，可以在保护农业数据隐私的同时提升跨农场模型性能，具备实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence has shown to help improve agricultural practices, yet adoption remains limited: 69% of U.S. farmers have privacy concerns with sharing their data, and these concerns must be addressed before adoption is widespread. While Federated Learning has been demonstrated to protect privacy at scale for other sectors, deploying a system for agriculture comes with its own set of challenges; the problem necessitates a system that can protect farmer data and identities while preserving model utility, runs on commodity hardware, and is resilient to fragile rural infrastructure. To address these concerns, we introduce the Private Computation Space (PCS), a deployed, open-source Machine Learning system to provision and process farmer data securely. We design a system tailored to an agricultural setting, with multi-cluster orchestration for reliability in rural areas with asynchronous Federated Learning (FL), Differential Privacy (DP), and Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs), to allow farms to participate in the framework while keeping their data private. We evaluate the system on two deployed workloads: monitoring nitrogen with living plant sensors in NY for six months and predicting evapotranspiration from weather stations in CA for ten months. Our evaluation finds a Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC) of 0.71 and $R^2$ accuracy of 0.84 for the respective workloads, improving the worst single-site model accuracy by 22.4% and 9.1%, respectively, while preserving privacy.

</details>


### [64] [Type-Directed, Secure-by-Construction Enclave Partitioning for LLVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02048)
*Wesley B. Nuzzo,Samuel Dodson,Benjamin Houle,Tarakaram Gollamudi,Anitha Gollamudi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SPLITR，通过从SIR到SIREN的类型驱动、类型保持编译，自动将低级LLVM风格程序划分为安全的非飞地与飞地部分，在无需手工划分的情况下实现信息流安全、粗粒度内存安全和可控的边界管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE能够隔离代码和数据，但无法单独保证信息流安全；LLVM类低级语言中的指针操纵和非结构化控制流进一步增加了安全验证难度。传统手工划分飞地与非飞地代码成本高、易出错，且难以精细控制可信计算基、边界转换和数据移动。

Method: 首先构建基于LLVM IR的飞地无关演算SIR，并设计面向低级攻击者的宽松类型系统，将信息流控制与安全感知的粗粒度内存安全结合。随后扩展为飞地感知演算SIREN，使其能够抵抗可观察任意非飞地内存的更强攻击者。最后设计从SIR到SIREN的类型驱动、类型保持编译器，自动生成安全的飞地划分，并支持对主机—飞地边界进行细粒度控制。

Result: 在13个微基准和真实工作负载上进行了评估，包括SGXGauge中的应用，并在Intel SGX硬件上实现。系统可扩展至包含425953条LLVM IR指令的OpenSSL。通过优化目标可权衡飞地可信计算基规模、主机—飞地转换次数和边界数据移动；在OpenSSL中，面向转换次数优化将转换数从393次降至187次。短时任务的运行时开销主要来自固定飞地成本，而长时任务能够更充分摊销该成本并接近原生性能。

Conclusion: SPLITR证明了类型系统与自动编译可以有效结合TEE，实现面向低级程序的端到端信息流安全和自动飞地划分。该方法降低了人工分区负担，并通过可优化的边界策略在安全性、性能和可信计算基规模之间提供实用权衡。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) provide hardware-supported isolation through enclaves that protect code and data independently of software abstractions. However, TEEs alone cannot enforce information-flow security. This problem is further aggravated in LLVM-like low-level languages that allow unrestricted pointer manipulation and unstructured control flow. Moreover, using TEEs effectively typically requires manually partitioning applications into enclave and non-enclave components, a process that is labor-intensive, error-prone, and lacks fine-grained control.
  We address these challenges with a three-step approach. First, we formalize SIR, an enclave-oblivious calculus based on LLVM IR, equipped with a novel permissive type system that enforces security against low-level attackers. To obtain meaningful guarantees, SIR combines information-flow control with security-aware coarse-grained memory safety. Second, we extend SIR to SIREN, an enclave-aware calculus that enforces noninterference against stronger attackers capable of observing arbitrary non-enclave memory. Third, we develop a type-driven, type-preserving compilation from SIR to SIREN that automatically produces secure enclave-aware programs, eliminating manual partitioning while providing fine-grained control over host-enclave boundaries.
  We implement and evaluate SPLITR on thirteen microbenchmarks and real-world workloads, including applications from SGXGauge, on Intel SGX hardware. SPLITR scales to OpenSSL (425,953 LLVM IR instructions) and supports multiple objectives that expose trade-offs among enclave TCB size, host-enclave transitions, and boundary data movement. For OpenSSL, optimizing for transitions reduces them from 393 to 187. Runtime overhead is dominated by fixed enclave costs for short-running workloads, whereas long-running applications better amortize these costs and approach native performance.

</details>


### [65] [Ranked by the Matcher: A Reproducibility Audit of Knowledge Graph Extraction from Threat Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01671)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文指出，威胁情报三元组抽取系统的F1分数高度依赖匹配协议，导致系统排名和结论不稳定；同时，确定性验证规则可能因与特定抽取器的设计约定绑定而产生偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常直接依据公开的三元组F1分数选择工具，但不同论文对预测三元组与金标准的匹配方式缺乏统一标准，且部分匹配规则难以复现。这可能使系统间比较失真，也掩盖验证组件、模型骨干和推理解码策略的实际影响。

Method: 研究者重新评估了10个系统在共享文档上的输出，使用8种匹配协议比较结果，并在GRID的378条外部校准数据上测试词法、嵌入、蕴含和LLM匹配器与多评审者裁决的一致性。随后构建CTIForge，使抽取结果保持字节级不变，仅改变确定性验证层，以分离验证规则的影响，并测试7种部署配置。

Result: 在45组系统两两比较中，有11组排名发生反转；同一预测集在不同协议下的F1范围为0.16至0.70。机械匹配器与多评审者裁决的一致率均不超过71%，LLM评判器达到86%。验证层使4个托管模型骨干的精确率均提升，却使3个离线模型骨干的精确率均下降；同时，明确质疑实体类型的操作增加约2.8倍。

Conclusion: 三元组F1不能脱离匹配协议解读，公开分数之间的直接比较存在明显风险。验证规则的效果并非普适，可能受模型骨干、解码方式、后端提示和开发时所针对的抽取器约定影响。论文通过CTIForge、协议套件和逐三元组审计记录，促进可复现、可审计的评估。

Abstract: Security teams and researchers choose knowledge-graph extraction tooling for threat reports on the strength of published triple-F1 scores, yet those scores depend on how predicted triples are matched to gold annotations. We could reimplement the stated matching rule for only five of twelve inspected systems. Re-scoring ten system outputs on shared documents under eight protocols reverses eleven of forty-five pairwise orderings; one fixed prediction set spans 0.16-0.70 F1. On GRID's external 378-item calibration set, no mechanical matcher (lexical, embedding, or entailment) agrees with multi-reviewer adjudication above 71%, whereas an LLM judge reaches 86%. To separate component effects from matcher rewards, we build CTIForge, whose deterministic validation layer can vary while extraction is held byte-identical. Across seven tested deployment configurations, validation raises precision for all four hosted backbones and lowers it for all three offline backbones. Because backbone, decoding, and backend-specific prompting covary, this is a descriptive split rather than an isolated serving effect. It coincides with a roughly 2.8-fold increase in actions explicitly disputing entity type, consistent with hand-written rules encoding the conventions of the extractor against which they were developed. We release the pipeline, protocol suite, and per-triple audit records.

</details>


### [66] [Skill-as-API: Confidential Multi-Agent Coordination for Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01677)
*Ziwei Zhao,Yu Gu,Haojun Liang,Chen Zhang,Xizhi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Skill-as-API 协调协议，将智能体技能封装在所有者进程内部，仅通过网络公开名称、描述、类型化输入输出模式和信任等级，从协议层保护技能实现及私有提示词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MCP、A2A 等协议虽然在服务端运行技能实现，但会向协作方公开技能描述和类型化模式，无法隐藏技能存在，也无法保证封装的系统提示词不被传输。应用层隐私过滤通常只能在模型生成敏感内容后进行拦截，因此需要一种在协议层实施的结构化保护机制。

Method: 设计 Skill-as-API 协调协议，将技能主体通过闭包保留在所有者进程中，网络仅传输受限的公开视图，包括技能名称、描述、类型化输入输出模式和信任等级。协议包含四层访问控制机制，以结构性方式缩小提示词注入面，而非依赖生成后的内容过滤。作者还基于 XMTP 提供开源 Python 实现，并通过软件工程案例验证多智能体协作流程。

Result: 实现支持跨大陆热重连，延迟为 1.8 至 2.9 秒。在包含三个智能体的拉取请求评审案例中，各智能体能够协同完成代码审查，同时保留各自专有分析提示词的所有权，且技能主体不跨网络传输。

Conclusion: Skill-as-API 通过将技能实现与私有提示词封装在本地进程、仅暴露受控接口信息，为多智能体协作提供了协议层隐私保护方案。结果表明，该方法在保持实际协作能力和可接受通信延迟的同时，能够降低技能知识产权泄露及提示词注入风险，但其安全性和通用性仍需在更多协议、任务类型和对抗环境中评估。

Abstract: AI coding agents are evolving from solitary tools into collaborative teammates that discover and invoke one another's specialized skills. But the coordination channel itself can leak a skill's intellectual property. Protocols such as MCP and A2A run implementations server-side, yet they still publish each skill's description and typed schemas to every peer, offer no way to hide a skill's existence, and cannot guarantee that a wrapped system prompt stays off the wire. Application-layer privacy filters help, but act only after the model has decided to emit sensitive text. We take a complementary, protocol-layer route: Skill-as-API, a coordination protocol whose public view of a skill is limited to its name, description, typed input/output schemas, and trust tier. The skill body is closure-captured in the owner's process and never crosses the wire. Four layers add access control and narrow the prompt-injection surface structurally rather than by filtering content. We provide an open-source Python implementation over XMTP with 1.8-2.9 s cross-continent hot-reconnect latency, and a software-engineering case study in which three agents coordinate a pull-request review while each retains ownership of its proprietary analysis prompts.

</details>


### [67] [Public-Sharing Labels and Verbatim Field Egress in an MCP-to-A2A Agent Configuration: A Controlled Multi-Model Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01693)
*Arpan Kumar Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究在一个受控的 MCP→A2A 串联配置中，测量模型是否会在外发消息中逐字泄露记录中的敏感字段。结果显示：机密标签与无标签条件均接近零泄露，因地板效应无法判断机密标签是否具有额外保护作用；“PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE”标签与更高的逐字外发相关，但该现象高度依赖模型，且仅能说明该单一配置下的描述性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP 工具调用和 A2A 委托通常被分别评估，但单个代理同时使用两者时的安全行为不能由分离评估直接推出。研究旨在通过可复现的端到端实验，检验记录标签如何关联到跨 MCP 与 A2A 链路的内容外发行为。

Method: 实验采用预注册并冻结的三臂设计：10 个记录场景分别设置 CONFIDENTIAL、无标签和 PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE 三种表头，六项实质记录值在各臂间保持字节级一致。测试床驱动真实模型宿主，经本地 MCP 和本地 A2A 链路生成有序事件轨迹；每次试验仅允许一次受限决策。结果变量为外发消息中是否逐字出现任一实质记录值，使用确定性精确规则评分，不采用 LLM 评审。4 个模型、3 个实验臂、每臂4次重复，共480次试验；以场景为泛化单位，报告10个场景层面的均值、中位数和符号计数，不报告显著性检验或区间估计。

Result: CONFIDENTIAL 相对无标签的差异在所有模型中均不确定，且两组泄露率均处于或接近零，因此存在明显地板效应，不能据此推断机密标签无保护作用。PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE 相对无标签则呈现更高逐字外发的描述性关联，但模型差异显著：Claude Sonnet 5 的增幅强且在全部10个场景中一致，场景均值差为+0.800，主要反映其是否进行整体转发；一个 GPT-5.6 层级表现为中等关联但仍受地板效应限制；另一层级效应较小且中位数为0；第三个层级完全处于地板。代码、字节固定的事件轨迹和离线分析管线已作为公开工件发布。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可审计、无需 LLM 裁判的 MCP→A2A 端到端安全测量范式，并表明记录的“可公开分享”标签在该特定配置中可能与更高的逐字内容外发有关。然而，证据仅限于单一受控配置和所测试模型，不能作因果解释，也不能推广为 MCP、A2A 或标签机制的一般安全结论。机密标签的独立保护效应仍需在避免地板效应的任务设置中进一步检验。

Abstract: Safety properties assessed separately for Model Context Protocol (MCP) tool use and Agent2Agent (A2A) delegation need not describe behavior when one agent uses both. We measure one such behavior in a single controlled MCP-to-A2A configuration: a testbed drives a real-model host across a local MCP and a local A2A leg into an ordered event trace scored by exact deterministic rules (no LLM judge), one restricted decision per trial. In a pre-specified, frozen three-arm design, each of 10 record scenarios appears with a CONFIDENTIAL header, with no header, and with PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE; the six substantive record values are byte-identical across arms, and the outcome is verbatim occurrence of any of them in the outbound message. Four models x 3 arms x 4 repeats give 480 trials; the scenario is the unit of generalization, and we report the 10 scenario-level values (mean, median, sign counts), with no p-values or intervals. The confidential-minus-unlabeled contrast is inconclusive and floor-limited in every model (both arms at or near zero), so it does not show that confidential labels lack a protective effect. Adding PUBLIC - OK TO SHARE is descriptively associated with higher verbatim egress relative to the unlabeled baseline, with strong model dependence: strong and consistent for Claude Sonnet 5 (public-minus-unlabeled mean +0.800, all 10 scenarios; mostly an association with whether Claude relays at all), moderate but floor-limited for one GPT-5.6 tier, small (median 0) for another, and a complete floor for the third. This is an association in one configuration, not a causal or general effect. Code, byte-pinned traces, and the offline analysis pipeline are released as a public artifact.

</details>


### [68] [Hearing the Whispers: Black-Box Membership Inference Attacks on Finetuned TTS Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01723)
*Kunlin Cai,Kaiyuan Zhang,Zihang Xiang,Jinghuai Zhang,Abeer Alwan,Fnu Suya,Yuan Tian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向文本到语音（TTS）模型的黑盒成员推断攻击框架，从说话人级和语音记录级识别模型训练成员，揭示微调TTS模型存在严重隐私泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS基础模型在私有语音数据上微调后，可能泄露说话人身份及语音内容。现有黑盒成员推断方法在TTS场景下面临双重条件查询空间复杂、缺乏有效查询判据，以及语音多层次特征和时间变化导致表征比较不充分等问题。

Method: 框架包含查询生成和表征工程两个阶段。首先刻画由合成文本与参考语音共同决定的可行查询空间，并提出可评分范围（scorable extent）和记忆诱发能力（memorization elicitation）两个评价标准，对五类代表性查询进行比较，确定复述式查询（recitation）最有效。随后利用语音嵌入模型提取多层次表征，并对生成语音与目标语音进行时间对齐，以实现细粒度相似性比较和成员推断。实验覆盖CosyVoice2、F5-TTS、XTTS-v2三个TTS模型，以及VCTK和British Dialect两个数据集。

Result: 说话人级成员推断的AUC均高于0.80，在最有利设置下接近1.0；记录级AUC约为0.80至0.90。即使成员样本与非成员样本来自相同说话人，攻击仍保持有效。实验还发现，部分语音特征对模型记忆和隐私泄漏具有更高脆弱性。

Conclusion: 微调TTS模型会产生显著的说话人级和记录级隐私泄漏，现有黑盒攻击能够在缺乏模型内部信息的情况下有效识别训练成员。复述式查询、时间对齐及多层次语音表征是提升攻击性能的关键，也说明TTS模型隐私评估和防护需要同时关注身份与内容两个层面。

Abstract: Text-to-Speech (TTS) foundation models are increasingly fine-tuned on private datasets to synthesize highly personalized voices, introducing severe privacy risks by exposing both biometric identities and sensitive speech content. Existing black-box membership inference attacks (MIAs) follow a two-stage pipeline of query generation and representation engineering, both of which face unique challenges when adapted to TTS.
